```text
 4997  		// the label being what the page calls the item (SAVES, ROMS AND BIOS,
 4998  		// SETTINGS) -- so the page can say which parts finished and which did
 4999  		// not: a run where one did and one did not is a failure with that part's why,
 5000  		// never the last part's code over the first part's files (D-UI-028).
 5001  		std::string cmd;
 5002  		auto add = [&cmd](const std::string& label, const std::string& part)
 5003  		{
 5004  			if (cmd.empty())
 5005  				cmd = "rc=0";
 5006  			cmd += " ; _t=0 ; { " + part + " ; } || _t=$? ; echo \">>> tier " + label + "|$_t\" ; [ \"$_t\" = 0 ] || rc=$_t";
 5007  		};
 5008  
 5009  		// A settings restore rewrites the configuration and restarts the
 5010  		// device, so it cannot be one link in a chain -- anything after it
 5011  		// would never run. The journey marker is what carries the rest
 5012  		// across the restart -- and backuptool sets it (--then-cloud), after
 5013  		// its extract has been verified, where this used to touch it before
 5014  		// the restore ran: a download that never happened, or a restore that
 5015  		// failed, still produced YOUR SETTINGS WERE RESTORED at the next
 5016  		// boot (D-CLOUD-078).
 5017  		//
 5018  		// One tier with two parts, the settings tier of a backup read
 5019  		// backwards: the archive comes down, then backuptool puts it in
 5020  		// place -- and only if it arrived, so a download that failed is not
 5021  		// followed by an extract of whatever was already on the card. ES
 5022  		// announces the item and what the second part is busy with, because
 5023  		// backuptool prints nothing this page can use (the same reason the
 5024  		// backup tier announces its own).
 5025  		//
 5026  		// --no-restart, and the page restarts the device instead: backuptool
 5027  		// left to itself sleeps five seconds and reboots, which takes the
 5028  		// screen away at the exact moment it has the outcome on it. An older
 5029  		// backuptool ignores the option and restarts as it always did (#114).
 5030  		//
 5031  		// What else was ticked rides across the restart in a record of its own
 5032  		// (JourneyTiers, audit #307 PL-029): the start builds the continuation
 5033  		// from it, where it used to run everything whatever was ticked -- the
 5034  		// dialog promising the ticks, the continuation restoring ROMs and BIOS
 5035  		// over Wi-Fi to a player who had asked for saves. It is written on YES,
 5036  		// not before the question, so a NO leaves nothing behind. With nothing
 5037  		// else ticked there is nothing to continue: no marker is asked for, the
 5038  		// dialog does not promise one, and a record an earlier attempt left is
 5039  		// removed.
 5040  		if (!backup && wantSettings)
 5041  		{
 5042  			const bool rest = wantSaves || wantContent || wantMedia;
 5043  			add("SETTINGS", std::string("echo '>>> unit SETTINGS||' ; /usr/bin/cloud_restore --yes --system-only"
 5044  				" && { echo '>>> doing unpack' ; /usr/bin/backuptool restore")
 5045  				+ (rest ? " --then-cloud" : "") + " --no-restart ; }");
 5046  			cmd += " ; exit $rc";
 5047  			const std::string question = rest
 5048  				? _("RESTORE SETTINGS FIRST, THEN RESTART?\n\nYOUR EXISTING CONFIGURATION IS REPLACED. ANYTHING ELSE YOU TICKED IS RESTORED AFTER THE RESTART. WI-FI AND ACCOUNT PASSWORDS MUST BE RE-ENTERED.")
 5049  				: _("RESTORE SETTINGS, THEN RESTART?\n\nYOUR EXISTING CONFIGURATION IS REPLACED. WI-FI AND ACCOUNT PASSWORDS MUST BE RE-ENTERED.");
 5050  			window->pushGui(new GuiMsgBox(window, question, _("YES"),
 5051  				[window, s, cmd, rest, wantSaves, wantContent, wantMedia]
 5052  				{
 5053  					const std::string record = JourneyTiers::PATH;
 5054  					if (rest)
 5055  					{
 5056  						Utils::FileSystem::createDirectory(Utils::FileSystem::getParent(record));
 5057  						// This restore's ticks, or no record at all: an earlier
 5058  						// attempt's record at the same path was read as this one's
 5059  						// when the write failed (audit of the fixes, E2 gpt G-E2-01).
 5060  						// No record leaves the start with a marker and nothing to
 5061  						// read: it offers everything and its prompt names
 5062  						// everything. A record that can be neither replaced nor
 5063  						// removed stops the restore here, before anything changes.
 5064  						const JourneyTiers::Replaced replaced = JourneyTiers::replaceRecord(
 5065  							JourneyTiers::record(wantSaves, wantContent, wantMedia),
 5066  							[&record](const std::string& text) { return Utils::AtomicFile::writeText(record, text); },
 5067  							[&record]() { return Utils::FileSystem::removeFile(record); },
 5068  							[&record]() { return Utils::FileSystem::exists(record, false); });
 5069  						if (replaced == JourneyTiers::Replaced::NoRecord)
 5070  							LOG(LogWarning) << "restore: the journey record could not be written to " << record << "; the start will offer everything";
 5071  						else if (replaced == JourneyTiers::Replaced::OldRecordStands)
 5072  						{
 5073  							LOG(LogError) << "restore: an earlier journey record at " << record << " could be neither replaced nor removed; the restore was not started";
 5074  							s->close();
 5075  							window->pushGui(new GuiMsgBox(window, _("COULDN'T SAVE WHAT YOU TICKED, SO NOTHING WAS RESTORED."), _("OK")));
 5076  							return;
 5077  						}
 5078  					}
 5079  					else if (Utils::FileSystem::exists(record, false))
 5080  						Utils::FileSystem::removeFile(record);
 5081  					s->close();
 5082  					auto page = new GuiCloudTransfer(window, cmd, _("RESTORING SETTINGS FROM THE CLOUD"), 1);
 5083  					page->setCompletedAction([]
 5084  					{
 5085  						// The configuration on disk is no longer the one
 5086  						// this process holds. Re-read it before anything
 5087  						// can write the old values back over the restore:
 5088  						// ViewController::saveState() saves es_settings.cfg
 5089  						// on the way out whenever the player has moved to
 5090  						// another system since this boot, and that write
 5091  						// carries every other setting with it.
 5092  						Settings::getInstance()->loadFile();
 5093  						SystemConf::getInstance()->loadSystemConf();
 5094  						ViewController::configurationReplaced();
 5095  						Utils::Platform::quitES(Utils::Platform::QuitMode::REBOOT);
 5096  					}, _("RESTART"), _("PRESS ANY BUTTON TO RESTART"),
 5097  					   _("THIS DEVICE RESTARTS SO YOUR RESTORED SETTINGS TAKE EFFECT."));
 5098  					window->pushGui(page);
 5099  				}, _("NO"), nullptr));
 5100  			return;
 5101  		}
 5102  
 5103  		// Order matters on a backup: the settings archive is written last so
 5104  		// the copy that goes up is the current one.
 5105  		// --saves-only: without it the saves scripts run their settings-archive
 5106  		// phase too, and a run with SETTINGS unticked still moved the archive
 5107  		// under a SETTINGS BACKUP label the page then kept showing through the
 5108  		// ROMs (maintainer, 2026-09-06). The settings tier below is the only
 5109  		// thing that moves settings.
 5110  		if (wantSaves)
 5111  			add("SAVES", backup ? "/usr/bin/cloud_backup --yes --saves-only" : "/usr/bin/cloud_restore --yes --saves-only");
 5112  		if (wantContent || wantMedia)
 5113  			add(wantContent && wantMedia ? "ROMS, BIOS, AND GAME CONTENT" : wantContent ? "ROMS AND BIOS" : "GAME CONTENT",
 5114  			    (backup ? std::string("/usr/bin/cloud_content_backup --selected")
 5115  			            : std::string("/usr/bin/cloud_content_restore --selected"))
 5116  			    + cloudContentMode(wantContent, wantMedia));
 5117  		// The settings item announces itself before backuptool runs, and says
 5118  		// what it is doing while the archive is written: backuptool prints
 5119  		// nothing the page can use, and without these two lines the item sat
 5120  		// on PREPARING... over a spinner while every other item showed its
 5121  		// files (maintainer, 2026-09-09, D-UI-026). cloud_backup announces
 5122  		// ">>> unit SETTINGS||" again when its turn comes; the same label, so
 5123  		// the page does not count it twice.
 5124  		if (backup && wantSettings)
 5125  			add("SETTINGS", "echo '>>> unit SETTINGS||' ; echo '>>> doing archive' ; /usr/bin/backuptool backup >/dev/null 2>&1 && /usr/bin/cloud_backup --yes --system-only");
 5126  
 5127  		// How many items the page should expect (ITEM i OF n, D-UI-026): one
 5128  		// for saves, one per system the picker left ticked, one for settings.
 5129  		// The selection is the file cloud_content_restore --set-systems wrote
 5130  		// as the picker closed -- read here rather than asked for through the
 5131  		// script, whose startup runs rclone listremotes: a process on the UI
 5132  		// thread for a number the content script corrects anyway when it
 5133  		// announces its own count (it adds bios to what was ticked). The
 5134  		// settings item comes after the content phase, so that correction is
 5135  		// told to keep counting it.
 5136  		int items = (wantSaves ? 1 : 0) + (backup && wantSettings ? 1 : 0);
 5137  		int itemsAfterContent = 0;
 5138  		if (wantContent || wantMedia)
 5139  		{
 5140  			{
 5141  				std::vector<std::string> picked;
 5142  				cloudSelectionRead(CLOUD_CONTENT_SELECTION, picked);
 5143  				items += (int) picked.size();
 5144  			}
 5145  			itemsAfterContent = backup && wantSettings ? 1 : 0;
 5146  		}
 5147  
 5148  		// A screen, not a card. This is the flow that moves gigabytes, and the
 5149  		// card closes itself the moment the job ends -- so a restore somebody
 5150  		// walked away from left no trace but a log file. The quick save actions
 5151  		// in GAME SETTINGS keep the card, because those finish while you watch.
 5152  		if (!cmd.empty())
 5153  			cmd += " ; exit $rc";
 5154  
 5155  		const std::string title = up ? _("BACKING UP TO THE CLOUD") : _("RESTORING FROM THE CLOUD");
 5156  		s->close();
 5157  		w->pushGui(new GuiCloudTransfer(w, cmd, title, items, itemsAfterContent));
 5158  	};
 5159  
 5160  	// Which systems is a question only the per-system classes raise -- ROMS AND
 5161  	// BIOS and GAME CONTENT -- so it is a second step rather than a row sitting
 5162  	// there whether or not it applies, and the button says CONTINUE when there
 5163  	// is a step after it, which is the only honest label for a control that
 5164  	// does not yet perform the action.
 5165  	auto rebuildButtons = [s, window, backup, content, media, hasContent, run, perSystemLine, wholeDeviceLine]()
 5166  	{
 5167  		const bool staged = hasContent && (content->getState() || media->getState());
 5168  		s->getMenu().clearButtons();
 5169  		s->getMenu().addButton(_("BACK"), _("back"), [s] { s->close(); });
 5170  		s->getMenu().addButton(
 5171  			staged ? _("CONTINUE") : (backup ? _("BACK UP") : _("RESTORE")),
 5172  			staged ? _("choose systems") : (backup ? _("back up") : _("restore")),
 5173  			[window, staged, run, backup, content, media, perSystemLine, wholeDeviceLine]
 5174  			{
 5175  				// A holder of its own, on the stack: the verb closes the page
 5176  				// that holds every other one (the comment in run).
 5177  				const auto keep = run;
 5178  				if (staged)
 5179  					cloudScanContent(window, backup, content->getState(), media->getState(),
 5180  						[window, run, backup, content, media, perSystemLine, wholeDeviceLine]
 5181  						{
 5182  							cloudContentSystemPicker(window, [run] { (*run)(); },
 5183  								backup ? _("BACK UP") : _("RESTORE"), backup,
 5184  								content->getState(), media->getState(), perSystemLine(), wholeDeviceLine());
 5185  						});
 5186  				else
 5187  					(*keep)();
 5188  			});
 5189  	};
 5190  	rebuildButtons();
 5191  	if (hasContent)
 5192  	{
 5193  		content->setOnChangedCallback([rebuildButtons] { rebuildButtons(); });
 5194  		media->setOnChangedCallback([rebuildButtons] { rebuildButtons(); });
 5195  		// Each switch's callback holds rebuildButtons, which holds both
 5196  		// switches (and run, and the line builders, which hold them too): a
 5197  		// cycle that kept every closed form alive, with its switches, its
 5198  		// run and a pointer to the page it was built for (#308 8a gpt
 5199  		// F-ES-11). The page lets go of the callbacks as it closes, and the
 5200  		// rest goes with the page's rows. Owned by the page, as the picker's
 5201  		// rebuild is (es-code-traps.md, a button bar rebuilt from a button).
 5202  		s->onFinalize([content, media]
 5203  		{
 5204  			content->setOnChangedCallback(nullptr);
 5205  			media->setOnChangedCallback(nullptr);
 5206  		});
 5207  	}
 5208  
 5209  	window->pushGui(s);
 5210  }
 5211  
 5212  // The content scan, on a page of its own (#350): the content folder is
 5213  // settled first on a restore (above), then cloud_scan --content lists the
 5214  // cloud's systems in the classes ticked, and the systems page is built
 5215  // from its files. A scan that did not complete stays on its page with its
 5216  // why and TRY AGAIN beside CLOSE; one that did goes straight on, since the
 5217  // systems page is its outcome (GuiCloudTransfer::setAutoContinue). Scripts
 5218  // older than cloud_scan run the listing in the picker as they always did.
 5219  static void cloudScanContent(Window* window, bool backup, bool content, bool media, const std::function<void()>& then)
 5220  {
 5221  	if (!Utils::FileSystem::exists("/usr/bin/cloud_scan"))
 5222  	{
 5223  		cloudCheckChanged(window);
 5224  		return;
 5225  	}
 5226  	const std::string run = cloudNewCheckId();
 5227  	sCloudContentScanRun = run;
 5228  	sCloudContentScanMode = cloudContentMode(content, media);
 5229  	auto page = new GuiCloudTransfer(window, "/usr/bin/cloud_scan --content --run-id "
 5230  		+ cloudShellQuote(run) + sCloudContentScanMode, _("CHECKING YOUR CLOUD"), 1);
 5231  	page->setAutoContinue([window, run, then]
 5232  	{
 5233  		if (sCloudContentScanRun != run || !cloudScanStamped("content-done"))
 5234  		{
 5235  			cloudCheckChanged(window);
 5236  			return;
 5237  		}
 5238  		then();
 5239  	});
 5240  		window->pushGui(page);
 5241  }
 5242  
 5243  // Scan the selected folders before offering the transfers they support.
 5244  // A failed scan retains its reason and retry action on the scan page.
 5245  static void cloudOpenTransfer(Window* window, bool backup)
 5246  {
 5247  	if (!Utils::FileSystem::exists("/storage/.config/rclone/rclone.conf", false))
 5248  	{
 5249  		window->pushGui(new GuiMsgBox(window, _("NO CLOUD STORAGE IS SET UP ON THIS DEVICE YET.\n\nSET IT UP NOW?"), _("YES"),
 5250  			[window] { GuiMenu::openCloudAddRemote(window); }, _("NO"), nullptr));
 5251  		return;
 5252  	}
 5253  	if (!Utils::FileSystem::exists("/usr/bin/cloud_scan"))
 5254  	{
 5255  		cloudOpenTransferOptions(window, backup);
 5256  		return;
 5257  	}
 5258  	const std::string run = cloudNewCheckId();
 5259  	sCloudScanRun = run;
 5260  	auto page = new GuiCloudTransfer(window, "/usr/bin/cloud_scan --run-id " + cloudShellQuote(run), _("CHECKING YOUR CLOUD"), 3);
 5261  	page->setAutoContinue([window, backup, run]
 5262  	{
 5263  		if (sCloudScanRun != run || !cloudScanStamped("done"))
 5264  		{
 5265  			cloudCheckChanged(window);
 5266  			return;
 5267  		}
 5268  		cloudOpenTransferOptions(window, backup);
 5269  	});
 5270  	window->pushGui(page);
 5271  }
 5272  
 5273  // Step three: make this device match the cloud.
 5274  //
 5275  // The only cloud operation that deletes, so it previews first and the
 5276  // confirmation names what goes. The preview is a network round trip -- an
 5277  // rclone dry run per selected system -- so it runs behind GuiLoading rather
 5278  // than freezing the menu.
 5279  // What --match said and how it ended.
 5280  struct CloudMatchPreview
 5281  {
 5282  	std::vector<std::string> lines;
 5283  	int rc = -1;
 5284  };
 5285  
 5286  static void cloudOpenMatch(Window* window)
 5287  {
 5288  	window->pushGui(new GuiLoading<CloudMatchPreview>(
 5289  		window, _("COMPARING YOUR ROMS AND BIOS FILES WITH THE CLOUD"),
 5290  		[](auto gui)
 5291  		{
 5292  			CloudMatchPreview r;
 5293  			r.rc = ApiSystem::executeScriptLegacy("/usr/bin/cloud_content_restore --match",
 5294  				[&r](const std::string& line) { r.lines.push_back(line); }).second;
 5295  			return r;
 5296  		},
 5297  		[window](CloudMatchPreview result)
 5298  		{
 5299  			// The backend declines -- no network, an unreachable remote, an
 5300  			// empty content root, exclusions not loaded, no systems selected
 5301  			// for this device -- by exiting non-zero and printing why. Show
 5302  			// its words rather than inventing a summary of a refusal we did
 5303  			// not make: "No systems are selected for this device yet." used
 5304  			// to fall through to THIS DEVICE ALREADY MATCHES YOUR CLOUD,
 5305  			// which was false (#105).
 5306  			if (result.rc == CloudExit::NoNetwork)
 5307  			{
 5308  				window->pushGui(new GuiMsgBox(window, _("COULDN'T REACH YOUR CLOUD.\n\nTRY AGAIN WHEN YOU'RE ONLINE."), _("OK")));
 5309  				return;
 5310  			}
 5311  			bool refusal = false;
 5312  			std::string said;
 5313  			for (auto& raw : result.lines)
 5314  			{
 5315  				const std::string line = Utils::String::trim(raw);
 5316  				if (line.empty() || line.find('|') != std::string::npos)
 5317  					continue;   // a plan line, read below
 5318  				if (Utils::String::startsWith(line, "Refusing"))
 5319  					refusal = true;
 5320  				said += (said.empty() ? "" : "\n") + Utils::String::toUpper(line);
 5321  			}
 5322  			if (result.rc != 0 || refusal)
 5323  			{
 5324  				if (said.empty())
 5325  					said = _("COULDN'T READ YOUR CLOUD'S CONTENT.");
 5326  				window->pushGui(new GuiMsgBox(window, said + "\n\n" + _("NOTHING WAS CHANGED."), _("OK")));
 5327  				return;
 5328  			}
 5329  
 5330  			struct Change { std::string name; long files; long bytes; };
 5331  			std::vector<Change> changes;
 5332  			long totalFiles = 0, totalBytes = 0;
 5333  			for (auto& line : result.lines)
 5334  			{
 5335  				auto p = Utils::String::split(Utils::String::trim(line), '|', true);
 5336  				if (p.size() < 4)
 5337  					continue;
 5338  				long files = atol(p[2].c_str());
 5339  				if (files <= 0)
 5340  					continue;
 5341  				long bytes = atol(p[3].c_str());
 5342  				changes.push_back({ p[0], files, bytes });
 5343  				totalFiles += files;
 5344  				totalBytes += bytes;
 5345  			}
 5346  
 5347  			if (changes.empty())
 5348  			{
 5349  				window->pushGui(new GuiMsgBox(window,
 5350  					_("THIS DEVICE ALREADY MATCHES YOUR CLOUD.\n\nTHERE IS NOTHING TO REMOVE."), _("OK")));
 5351  				return;
 5352  			}
 5353  
 5354  			// Sizes are the transfer page's (GuiCloudTransfer::sizeLabel), as
 5355  			// on the picker rows and the done page this dialog leads to, so
 5356  			// the number a player agrees to here is the number they see
 5357  			// removed there. kiloBytesToString of a KB-rounded size read
 5358  			// "200.00 KB" -- two decimals that could only ever be zero (#85).
 5359  			std::string detail;
 5360  			for (auto& c : changes)
 5361  			{
 5362  				detail += Utils::String::toUpper(c.name) + "  -  "
 5363  					+ std::to_string(c.files) + " " + (c.files == 1 ? _("FILE") : _("FILES"));
 5364  				if (c.bytes > 0)
 5365  					detail += "  (" + GuiCloudTransfer::sizeLabel((unsigned long) c.bytes) + ")";
 5366  				detail += "\n";
 5367  			}
 5368  
 5369  			// Says what goes, what arrives, and what is never touched. The
 5370  			// third line matters most: this is the one action that can delete
 5371  			// a library, and somebody about to press YES needs to know their
 5372  			// saves are not in scope.
 5373  			std::string text = _("REMOVE") + std::string(" ") + std::to_string(totalFiles)
 5374  				+ " " + (totalFiles == 1 ? _("FILE FROM THIS DEVICE?") : _("FILES FROM THIS DEVICE?"));
 5375  			if (totalBytes > 0)
 5376  				text += "  (" + GuiCloudTransfer::sizeLabel((unsigned long) totalBytes) + ")";
 5377  			text += "\n\n" + detail
 5378  				+ "\n" + _("ANYTHING YOUR CLOUD HAS THAT THIS DEVICE DOES NOT IS DOWNLOADED AT THE SAME TIME.")
 5379  				+ "\n" + _("GAME SAVES, SAVE STATES, AND SCREENSHOTS ARE NEVER TOUCHED.");
 5380  
 5381  			window->pushGui(new GuiMsgBox(window, text, _("YES"), [window]
 5382  			{
 5383  				window->pushGui(new GuiCloudTransfer(window,
 5384  					"/usr/bin/cloud_content_restore --match --apply",
 5385  					_("MATCHING THIS DEVICE TO THE CLOUD")));
 5386  			}, _("NO"), nullptr));
 5387  		}));
 5388  }
 5389  
 5390  // The three rows of BACKUP AND RESTORE follow the transfer that is current
 5391  // (CloudTransferJob::current). Built for a page that could be left with
 5392  // its run going on (fork #187; the scan row under SCAN GAMES was the model,
 5393  // audit #186 PL-07); since D-UI-078 the transfer page is sat in, so while
 5394  // a run goes this hub is under it and is not ticked, and what these rows
 5395  // meet is the run's outcome, kept current until the outcome page has been
 5396  // dismissed (D-UI-070): LAST <date> - COMPLETED, then the row's own line
```

```text
 5412  // MultiLineMenuEntry::setDimmed does that (ComponentListFlags::dimmed, the
 5413  // same 0x50), as the offline scan row in GuiRetroAchievementsSettings.cpp
 5414  // uses it; the page's own subclass for it went (#308 8-es claude F-ES-25).
 5415  // It applied the dim at the next frame, where setDimmed applies it at once;
 5416  // nothing else differed.
 5417  
 5418  // The three rows, held weakly as every row on a page is, each with the line
 5419  // it returns to.
 5420  struct CloudHubRows
 5421  {
 5422  	struct Row
 5423  	{
 5424  		std::weak_ptr<MultiLineMenuEntry> entry;
 5425  		std::string idle;
 5426  	};
 5427  	Row backup, restore, match;
 5428  };
 5429  
 5430  // The line under the row that launched the run: in flight, the page's own
 5431  // words for it (GuiCloudTransfer::rowWords) with the item where the panel
 5432  // has room and the verb's word as the form that fits any panel (D-UI-035);
 5433  // ended, the outcome dated the way every LAST row is. The description is
 5434  // drawn in the menu's small font, in a row that spans the menu less its
 5435  // insets; 0.86 of the menu width is what a 640x480 frame showed the line
 5436  // to have (the scan row's measure).
 5437  static std::string cloudTransferRowLine(const std::shared_ptr<CloudTransferJob>& job)
 5438  {
 5439  	std::vector<std::string> candidates;
 5440  	if (!job->finished())
 5441  	{
 5442  		const GuiCloudTransfer::RowWords w = GuiCloudTransfer::rowWords(job);
 5443  		if (!w.item.empty())
 5444  			candidates.push_back(w.counted + "  ·  " + w.item);
 5445  		if (w.counted != w.word)
 5446  			candidates.push_back(w.counted);
 5447  		candidates.push_back(w.word);
 5448  	}
 5449  	else
 5450  	{
 5451  		const std::string outcome = GuiCloudTransfer::outcomeWord(job);
 5452  		candidates.push_back(cloudLastLabel(job->finishedAt()) + "  -  " + outcome);
 5453  		candidates.push_back(outcome);
 5454  	}
 5455  	auto theme = ThemeData::getMenuTheme();
 5456  	const float menuWidth = Renderer::ScreenSettings::fullScreenMenus()
 5457  		? (float) Renderer::getScreenWidth()
 5458  		: (float) Math::min((int) Renderer::getScreenHeight(), (int) (Renderer::getScreenWidth() * 0.90f));
 5459  	std::shared_ptr<Font> font = theme->TextSmall.font;
 5460  	return CloudText::chooseThatFits(candidates, menuWidth * 0.86f,
 5461  		[font](const std::string& t) { return font ? font->sizeText(t).x() : 0.0f; });
 5462  }
 5463  
 5464  static void cloudHubRefreshRow(CloudHubRows::Row& row, const std::shared_ptr<CloudTransferJob>& job, bool launchedHere)
 5465  {
 5466  	auto entry = row.entry.lock();
 5467  	if (!entry)
 5468  		return;
 5469  	std::string text = row.idle;
 5470  	bool dimmed = false;
 5471  	if (job != nullptr)
 5472  	{
 5473  		if (launchedHere)
 5474  			text = cloudTransferRowLine(job);
 5475  		else
 5476  			dimmed = true;
 5477  	}
 5478  	entry->setDimmed(dimmed);
 5479  	// Only when the words change: the refresher asks once a second, and a
 5480  	// line set to itself would still lay the row out again.
 5481  	if (text != entry->getDescription())
 5482  		entry->setDescription(text);
 5483  }
 5484  
 5485  static void cloudHubRefresh(const std::shared_ptr<CloudHubRows>& rows)
 5486  {
 5487  	const std::shared_ptr<CloudTransferJob> job = CloudTransferJob::current();
 5488  	const CloudText::TransferKind kind = job != nullptr
 5489  		? CloudText::transferKind(job->command()) : CloudText::TransferKind::Other;
 5490  	cloudHubRefreshRow(rows->backup,  job, kind == CloudText::TransferKind::Backup);
 5491  	cloudHubRefreshRow(rows->restore, job, kind == CloudText::TransferKind::Restore);
 5492  	cloudHubRefreshRow(rows->match,   job, kind == CloudText::TransferKind::Match);
 5493  }
 5494  
 5495  // The hub carries one component that is never drawn and never focused,
 5496  // asks once a second while the hub is the top page, and refreshes the rows
 5497  // -- which change their words only when they differ (cloudHubRefreshRow).
 5498  // Owned by the page (EXTRACHILDREN: GuiComponent's destructor deletes it),
 5499  // so it dies with the page; ticked by the page's update, which Window
 5500  // gives to the top page alone, so the transfer page over it pauses it and
 5501  // the rows catch up the second that page is dismissed -- which, with the
 5502  // page sat in (D-UI-078), is when the run has ended and its outcome is
 5503  // what there is to show. Built as the scan row's refresher was, for a run
 5504  // nobody was watching (#187).
 5505  class CloudHubRefresher : public GuiComponent
 5506  {
 5507  public:
 5508  	CloudHubRefresher(Window* window, const std::shared_ptr<CloudHubRows>& rows)
 5509  		: GuiComponent(window), mRows(rows), mElapsedMs(0)
 5510  	{
 5511  		setVisible(false);
 5512  		setExtraType(ExtraType::EXTRACHILDREN);
 5513  	}
 5514  
 5515  	void update(int deltaTime) override
 5516  	{
 5517  		GuiComponent::update(deltaTime);
 5518  		mElapsedMs += deltaTime;
 5519  		if (mElapsedMs < 1000)
 5520  			return;
 5521  		mElapsedMs = 0;
 5522  		cloudHubRefresh(mRows);
 5523  	}
 5524  
 5525  private:
 5526  	std::shared_ptr<CloudHubRows> mRows;
 5527  	int mElapsedMs;
 5528  };
 5529  
 5530  // A row of BACKUP AND RESTORE: cloudAddGatedEntry's row -- the label over
 5531  // one wrapped line, the action on A, the setup offer before a cloud is
 5532  // connected -- as an entry the page keeps a weak hold of once the cloud is
 5533  // connected, so its line can follow a run. A press while a run is current
 5534  // opens the run's page: there is nothing else it can do (the flock), and
 5535  // the page shows the run or its outcome.
 5536  static void cloudAddTransferRow(GuiSettings* s, Window* window, bool configured, CloudHubRows::Row& slot,
 5537  	const std::string& label, const std::string& description, const std::function<void()>& action)
 5538  {
 5539  	slot.idle = description;
 5540  	// One press for both branches: a row greyed at build time and set up
 5541  	// since runs this too (cloudAddGatedEntry re-checks at the press).
 5542  	const std::function<void()> press = [window, action]
 5543  	{
 5544  		if (const std::shared_ptr<CloudTransferJob> job = CloudTransferJob::current())
 5545  		{
 5546  			window->pushGui(new GuiCloudTransfer(window, job));
 5547  			return;
 5548  		}
 5549  		action();
 5550  	};
 5551  	if (!configured)
 5552  	{
 5553  		cloudAddGatedEntry(s, window, false, label, description, press);
 5554  		return;
 5555  	}
 5556  	auto entry = std::make_shared<MultiLineMenuEntry>(window, Utils::String::toUpper(label), description, true);
 5557  	slot.entry = entry;
 5558  	ComponentListRow row;
 5559  	row.addElement(entry, true);
 5560  	row.makeAcceptInputHandler(press);
 5561  	s->addRow(row);
 5562  }
 5563  
 5564  // The cloud hub. One page, reachable from one place.
 5565  //
 5566  // Cloud used to live in two menus: saves and content under GAME SETTINGS,
 5567  // connection and system settings under NETWORK SETTINGS. Two of the three data
 5568  // classes were in a different menu from the third, so "back up my handheld"
 5569  // meant knowing which half of the split you wanted before you could start.
 5570  // Direction first, then what moves -- the split the system actually keeps is
 5571  // the one the player is shown.
 5572  // A connection can change on a child page. Refresh its parent on return,
 5573  // after the input callback has unwound; never rebuild a live button bar.
 5574  class CloudConnectionsPage : public GuiSettings
 5575  {
 5576  public:
 5577  	CloudConnectionsPage(Window* window, const std::string& title, const std::function<void()>& refresh)
 5578  		: GuiSettings(window, title), mRefresh(refresh), mAlive(std::make_shared<bool>(true)),
 5579  		  mSnapshot(Utils::Platform::GetShOutput("/usr/bin/cloud_remote list")) { }
 5580  	~CloudConnectionsPage() override { *mAlive = false; }
 5581  	void topWindow(bool isTop) override
 5582  	{
 5583  		GuiSettings::topWindow(isTop);
 5584  		if (!isTop) return;
 5585  		auto alive = mAlive;
 5586  		mWindow->postToUiThread([this, alive]
 5587  		{
 5588  			if (!*alive || mWindow->peekGui() != this) return;
 5589  			const auto current = Utils::Platform::GetShOutput("/usr/bin/cloud_remote list");
 5590  			if (current == mSnapshot) return;
 5591  			mSnapshot = current;
 5592  			mRefresh();
 5593  			close();
 5594  		});
 5595  	}
 5596  private:
 5597  	std::function<void()> mRefresh;
 5598  	std::shared_ptr<bool> mAlive;
 5599  	std::string mSnapshot;
 5600  };
 5601  
 5602  void GuiMenu::openCloud(Window* window, bool onFolderRow)
 5603  {
 5604  	const bool configured = Utils::FileSystem::exists("/storage/.config/rclone/rclone.conf", false);
 5605  	auto s = new CloudConnectionsPage(window, _("CLOUD"), [window] { GuiMenu::openCloud(window); });
 5606  
 5607  	// The three rows follow the current transfer's outcome (D-UI-070; the
 5608  	// page is sat in since D-UI-078): see cloudAddTransferRow and the
 5609  	// refresher above.
 5610  	auto rows = std::make_shared<CloudHubRows>();
 5611  	s->addGroup(_("BACKUP AND RESTORE"));
 5612  	cloudAddTransferRow(s, window, configured, rows->backup, _("BACK UP TO THE CLOUD"),
 5613  		_("SEND THIS DEVICE'S DATA TO YOUR CLOUD."),
 5614  		[window] { cloudOpenTransfer(window, true); });
 5615  	cloudAddTransferRow(s, window, configured, rows->restore, _("RESTORE FROM THE CLOUD"),
 5616  		_("BRING DATA BACK ONTO THIS DEVICE."),
 5617  		[window] { cloudOpenTransfer(window, false); });
 5618  
 5619  	// The third action, and the only one that deletes. Below the two safe ones
 5620  	// deliberately: a destructive row above RESTORE would be the one a thumb
 5621  	// reaches first.
 5622  	if (Utils::FileSystem::exists("/usr/bin/cloud_content_restore"))
 5623  	{
 5624  		// Two lines (D-UI-023), and the same two as its siblings: a label and a
 5625  		// line saying what it does. This is the one row in the group that removes
 5626  		// something, so that is the line -- the preview then names every ROM.
 5627  		// It briefly carried its last-run stamp instead, which read as a third
 5628  		// kind of row beside two submenus (maintainer, 2026-09-06).
 5629  		cloudAddTransferRow(s, window, configured, rows->match, _("MATCH THIS DEVICE TO THE CLOUD"),
 5630  			_("REMOVE ROMS YOUR CLOUD NO LONGER HAS."), [window] { cloudOpenMatch(window); });
 5631  	}
 5632  	// Once now, so the first frame is right, then once a second while the
 5633  	// page is up.
 5634  	cloudHubRefresh(rows);
 5635  	s->addChild(new CloudHubRefresher(window, rows));
 5636  
 5637  	s->addGroup(_("SAVE MANAGEMENT"));
 5638  
 5639  	// The line under each toggle is how its sync last went, read from the
 5640  	// whole-run stamp ThreadedCloudSync leaves per cause (fork #94). The
 5641  	// card that shows the run is gone seconds after it ends, and a startup
 5642  	// sync runs while the player is looking at the boot splash or not at the
 5643  	// device at all -- so this row is where "did it run?" gets answered, and
 5644  	// it answers whether the toggle is on now or not: a stamp is a fact about
 5645  	// a run that happened. It replaced REQUIRES NETWORK ACCESS, which the
 5646  	// outcome says better when it matters (SKIPPED, NO NETWORK). Two lines,
 5647  	// never three (D-UI-023).
 5648  	auto cloud_startup = std::make_shared<SwitchComponent>(window);
 5649  	cloud_startup->setState(SystemConf::getInstance()->get("cloudsaves.startup") == "1");
 5650  	s->addWithDescription(_("SYNC SAVES DURING STARTUP"), cloudLastRunDetail("sync-startup"), cloud_startup);
 5651  	cloud_startup->setOnChangedCallback([cloud_startup] {
 5652  		SystemConf::getInstance()->set("cloudsaves.startup", cloud_startup->getState() ? "1" : "0");
 5653  		SystemConf::getInstance()->saveSystemConf();
 5654  	});
 5655  	auto cloud_gameexit = std::make_shared<SwitchComponent>(window);
 5656  	cloud_gameexit->setState(SystemConf::getInstance()->get("cloudsaves.gameexit") == "1");
 5657  	s->addWithDescription(_("SYNC SAVES WHEN EXITING A GAME"), cloudLastRunDetail("sync-exit"), cloud_gameexit);
 5658  	cloud_gameexit->setOnChangedCallback([cloud_gameexit] {
 5659  		SystemConf::getInstance()->set("cloudsaves.gameexit", cloud_gameexit->getState() ? "1" : "0");
 5660  		SystemConf::getInstance()->saveSystemConf();
 5661  	});
 5662  
 5663  	s->addGroup(_("CLOUD STORAGE SETUP"));
 5664  	if (configured)
 5665  	{
 5666  		// Who this device is connected to, and whether it answers.
 5667  		//
 5668  		// The page offered to connect a provider and never said one already
 5669  		// was: "there's nowhere where it shows what cloud backend you
 5670  		// currently have, which seems like a miss" (maintainer, 2026-09-10,
 5671  		// fork #110). The name is the player's own label for the remote when
 5672  		// they gave it one, otherwise the provider's name.
 5673  		//
 5674  		// One cloud_setup --info for the page: it runs rclone listremotes, a
 5675  		// route lookup and systemctl, synchronously on the interface thread,
 5676  		// and the page asked it three times -- once for a name nothing read
 5677  		// (#308 8-es claude F-ES-03).
 5678  		std::map<std::string, std::string> info = cloudSetupInfo();
 5679  		const std::string provider = CloudText::providerLabel(info["REMOTE_TYPE"]);
 5680  		if (!provider.empty())
 5681  			s->addWithLabel(_("CONNECTED TO"), std::make_shared<TextComponent>(window, provider,
 5682  				ThemeData::getMenuTheme()->Text.font, ThemeData::getMenuTheme()->Text.color));
 5683  
 5684  		// The thing to reach for when a sync has just failed: does the cloud
 5685  		// answer right now? Behind GuiLoading, because it is a network round
 5686  		// trip -- bounded by the script (a single listing, 10 s to connect,
 5687  		// 30 s to answer), so the page cannot hang on it.
 5688  		s->addWithDescription(_("CHECK CONNECTION"), _("SEE WHETHER YOUR CLOUD ANSWERS RIGHT NOW."), nullptr,
 5689  			[window, provider]
 5690  			{
 5691  				window->pushGui(new GuiLoading<int>(window, _("CHECKING YOUR CLOUD..."),
 5692  					[](auto gui)
 5693  					{
 5694  						return ApiSystem::executeScriptLegacy("/usr/bin/cloud_setup --check", [](const std::string) {}).second;
 5695  					},
 5696  					[window, provider](int rc)
 5697  					{
 5698  						const std::string who = provider.empty() ? _("YOUR CLOUD") : provider;
 5699  						std::string text;
 5700  						if (rc == 0)
 5701  							text = who + " " + _("ANSWERED. YOU'RE CONNECTED.");
 5702  						else if (rc == 1)
 5703  							text = _("NO CLOUD STORAGE IS SET UP ON THIS DEVICE YET.");
 5704  						else
 5705  							text = _("COULDN'T REACH") + " " + who + ".\n\n"
 5706  								+ _("CHECK WI-FI, THEN TRY AGAIN. IF IT KEEPS FAILING, SIGN IN AGAIN UNDER CLOUD CONNECTIONS.");
 5707  						window->pushGui(new GuiMsgBox(window, text, _("OK")));
 5708  					}));
 5709  			}, "", false, true);
 5710  
 5711  
 5712  		// Each data root keeps its own selection. Returning from the editor
 5713  		// rebuilds this hub at the same row, so the next press stays here.
 5714  		const std::string syncpath = info["SAVES_REMOTE"];
 5715  		s->addWithDescription(_("CHANGE CLOUD FOLDER"),
 5716  			_("YOUR SAVES ARE IN:") + " " + syncpath,
 5717  			nullptr, [window, s] { cloudOpenFolderSettings(window, [window, s] { GuiMenu::openCloud(window, true); s->close(); }); },
 5718  			"", onFolderRow, true);
 5719  		s->addWithDescription(_("CHECK CLOUD FOLDERS"), _("CHECK THE FOLDERS YOU CHOOSE."), nullptr,
 5720  			[window] { cloudSetupShowDoneStep(window, "", nullptr); }, "", false, true);
 5721  	}
 5722  	s->addWithDescription(_("CLOUD CONNECTIONS"),
 5723  		_("ADD, CHOOSE, OR MANAGE YOUR CONNECTIONS."),
 5724  		nullptr, [window] { GuiMenu::openCloudConnections(window); }, "", false, true);
 5725  
 5726  	// Only while there is a restore to finish.
 5727  	// Read uncached: exists() remembers its answer for the session, and this
 5728  	// marker is written by a restore that ran after boot -- a cached "absent"
 5729  	// from startup hid the row until the next restart (guest b, 2026-09-13).
 5730  	if (Utils::FileSystem::exists("/storage/.config/.restore-finish-pending", false))
 5731  	{
 5732  		// One line under the label on both panels (D-UI-023; the budget is
 5733  		// noted at openRestoreRelink's DEVICE PASSWORD row).
 5734  		// FINISH from here finishes the restore, as it does from NETWORK
 5735  		// SETTINGS: the row exists only while the marker does, and passing
 5736  		// false -- from when the row was always there -- left the marker, so
 5737  		// the page came back at the next start after FINISH (#308 8a gpt
 5738  		// F-ES-06).
 5739  		s->addWithDescription(_("FINISH RESTORE PROCESS"),
 5740  			_("RE-ENTER THE PASSWORDS BACKUPS LEAVE OUT (WI-FI, ACCOUNTS, DEVICE)."), nullptr,
 5741  			[window] { GuiMenu::openRestoreRelink(window, true); }, "", false, true);
 5742  	}
 5743  
 5744  	window->pushGui(s);
 5745  }
 5746  
 5747  void GuiMenu::openGamesSettings()
 5748  {
 5749  	Window* window = mWindow;
 5750  
 5751  	auto s = new GuiSettings(mWindow, _("GAME SETTINGS").c_str());
 5752  
 5753  	s->addGroup(_("TOOLS"));
```

```text
 6245  static std::map<std::string, std::string> cloudSetupInfo()
 6246  {
 6247  	std::map<std::string, std::string> info;
 6248  	// executeScriptLegacy keeps line boundaries; GetShOutput joins all
 6249  	// output lines together, which breaks the key=*** parse.
 6250  	// Local -- `ip route get`, systemctl, rclone listremotes -- and read
 6251  	// while pages are being built, so bounded: the route lookup is a
 6252  	// netlink call a wedged Wi-Fi driver can hold (fork #103), and rclone's
 6253  	// start-up is the rest of the cost.
 6254  	for (auto& line : ApiSystem::executeScriptLegacy("timeout 10 /usr/bin/cloud_setup --info"))
 6255  	{
 6256  		auto pos = line.find('=');
 6257  		if (pos != std::string::npos)
 6258  			info[line.substr(0, pos)] = Utils::String::trim(line.substr(pos + 1));
 6259  	}
 6260  	return info;
 6261  }
 6262  
 6263  
 6264  // A body-text row: never selectable, small theme font, padded to align
 6265  // with the selectable rows. `accent` renders the accent color used for
 6266  // values and commands.
 6267  static void cloudSetupAddInfoRow(GuiSettings* s, Window* window, const std::string& text, bool accent = false)
 6268  {
 6269  	auto theme = ThemeData::getMenuTheme();
 6270  	ComponentListRow row;
 6271  	row.selectable = false;
 6272  	// Standard text size, not the small one: these rows carry a step's own
 6273  	// content - a status, an instruction, the command to type - so they sit
 6274  	// at the same weight as the action rows beside them. Only genuinely
 6275  	// subordinate text (a row's description) drops to the small size, which
 6276  	// MultiLineMenuEntry already does for us.
 6277  	auto tc = std::make_shared<TextComponent>(window, text, theme->Text.font, accent ? theme->Text.selectedColor : theme->Text.color);
 6278  	tc->setPadding(CLOUD_SETUP_ROW_PADDING);
 6279  	row.addElement(tc, true);
 6280  	s->addRow(row);
 6281  }
 6282  
 6283  
 6284  // A cloud action row. Before a remote exists the row stays visible but
 6285  // dimmed and offers the setup flow instead of its action - hiding it
 6286  // would tell the player nothing about what configuring a remote buys
 6287  // them. Shared by every row on the cloud page so they behave identically.
 6288  static void cloudAddGatedEntry(GuiSettings* s, Window* window, bool configured, const std::string& label, const std::string& description, const std::function<void()>& action)
 6289  {
 6290  	if (configured)
 6291  	{
 6292  		s->addWithDescription(label, description, nullptr, action, "", false, true);
 6293  		return;
 6294  	}
 6295  
 6296  	ComponentListRow row;
 6297  	auto entry = std::make_shared<MultiLineMenuEntry>(window, Utils::String::toUpper(label), description, true);
 6298  	// Dimmed by the entry on every frame, not by a colour set once here:
 6299  	// ComponentList recolours every element from the theme each frame, and
 6300  	// a row dimmed at construction was back to full colour by the first one
 6301  	// -- these rows never once rendered dim (fork #182).
 6302  	entry->setDimmed(true);
 6303  	row.addElement(entry, true);
 6304  	// Configured is decided when the page is built, and setup is opened from
 6305  	// this very row: CLOUD SETUP COMPLETE's FINISH closes back to the page it
 6306  	// came from, built before rclone.conf existed, whose rows went on
 6307  	// offering the setup just finished until the page was reopened (audit
 6308  	// #307 PL-062). So the press asks again -- a stat, read uncached -- and a
 6309  	// row that finds the cloud set up does what it says and stops drawing
 6310  	// dim. Every page with these rows gets it, which reopening one page from
 6311  	// FINISH would not. Weak: the row holds the entry, not the other way.
 6312  	std::weak_ptr<MultiLineMenuEntry> weakEntry = entry;
 6313  	row.makeAcceptInputHandler([window, weakEntry, action]
 6314  	{
 6315  		if (Utils::FileSystem::exists("/storage/.config/rclone/rclone.conf", false))
 6316  		{
 6317  			if (auto shown = weakEntry.lock())
 6318  				shown->setDimmed(false);
 6319  			action();
 6320  			return;
 6321  		}
 6322  		window->pushGui(new GuiMsgBox(window, _("NO CLOUD STORAGE IS SET UP ON THIS DEVICE YET.\n\nSET IT UP NOW?"), _("YES"),
 6323  			[window] { GuiMenu::openCloudAddRemote(window); },
 6324  			_("NO"), nullptr));
 6325  	});
 6326  	s->addRow(row);
 6327  }
 6328  
 6329  // A block of prose that ES wraps for us. Hand-splitting a sentence across
 6330  // fixed rows breaks on a narrower screen and in every translation, so
 6331  // anything longer than a line goes through MultiLineMenuEntry - the same
 6332  // component every description row uses - with wrapping switched on.
 6333  static void cloudSetupAddProse(GuiSettings* s, Window* window, const std::string& title, const std::string& body)
 6334  {
 6335  	ComponentListRow row;
 6336  	row.selectable = false;
 6337  	// ComponentList insets selectable rows but not unselectable ones, so a
 6338  	// prose row is flush left unless it pads itself -- misaligned against the
 6339  	// group headers and value rows around it.
 6340  	auto entry = std::make_shared<MultiLineMenuEntry>(window, title, body, true);
 6341  	entry->setPadding(CLOUD_SETUP_ROW_PADDING);
 6342  	row.addElement(entry, true);
 6343  	s->addRow(row);
 6344  }
 6345  
 6346  // Several lines of body text stacked as one component, so they can sit in a
 6347  // column beside something tall. ComponentList lays a row's elements out left
 6348  // to right, never top to bottom, so a stack has to be a single element -- and
 6349  // MultiLineMenuEntry is not it: its second line is deliberately smaller and
 6350  // dimmer, which is wrong for an address and a code that both have to be read
 6351  // off the screen and typed.
 6352  static std::shared_ptr<ComponentGrid> cloudSetupTextStack(Window* window,
 6353  	const std::vector<std::pair<std::string, bool>>& lines)
 6354  {
 6355  	auto theme = ThemeData::getMenuTheme();
 6356  	auto grid = std::make_shared<ComponentGrid>(window, Vector2i(1, (int)lines.size()));
 6357  
 6358  	float height = 0;
 6359  	for (int i = 0; i < (int)lines.size(); i++)
 6360  	{
 6361  		auto tc = std::make_shared<TextComponent>(window, lines[i].first, theme->Text.font,
 6362  			lines[i].second ? theme->Text.selectedColor : theme->Text.color);
 6363  		tc->setPadding(CLOUD_SETUP_ROW_PADDING);
 6364  		tc->setVerticalAlignment(ALIGN_TOP);
 6365  		grid->setEntry(tc, Vector2i(0, i), false, true);
 6366  
 6367  		// Absolute row heights, so the stack keeps its shape when
 6368  		// ComponentList later resizes it to the width left over beside the
 6369  		// image. A percentage would re-divide whatever height it was given.
 6370  		const float lineHeight = tc->getSize().y();
 6371  		grid->setRowHeight(i, lineHeight, false);
 6372  		height += lineHeight;
 6373  	}
 6374  
 6375  	grid->setSize(0, height);
 6376  	return grid;
 6377  }
 6378  
 6379  // An image with text beside it rather than under it.
 6380  //
 6381  // The sign-in page cannot scroll: every row on it is unselectable, so
 6382  // ComponentList never moves its camera and anything past the fold is
 6383  // unreachable rather than merely off-screen (which is why a QR stacked above
 6384  // the address cut the address in half). Vertical space is therefore the
 6385  // binding constraint, and a square code sitting alone on a wide row wastes
```

```text
 6414  static void cloudSetupAddQrRow(GuiSettings* s, Window* window, const std::string& imagePath,
 6415  	const std::vector<std::pair<std::string, bool>>& lines)
 6416  {
 6417  	ComponentListRow row;
 6418  	row.selectable = false;
 6419  
 6420  	// A share of the screen, not of the menu: the menu is capped at the
 6421  	// screen height, so this stays the same physical size on a wide panel
 6422  	// and a square one, and always leaves the majority of the width to text.
 6423  	const float qrEdge = Renderer::getScreenHeight() * 0.28f;
 6424  
 6425  	auto qr = std::make_shared<UntintedImageComponent>(window);
 6426  	qr->setOpacity(255);
 6427  	// Padding before setMaxSize -- ImageComponent folds it into the size it
 6428  	// reports, which is what the row measures the column by.
 6429  	qr->setPadding(CLOUD_SETUP_ROW_PADDING);
 6430  	qr->setImage(imagePath, false, MaxSizeInfo(qrEdge, qrEdge));
 6431  	qr->setMaxSize(qrEdge, qrEdge);
 6432  	row.addElement(qr, false);
 6433  
 6434  	row.addElement(cloudSetupTextStack(window, lines), true);
 6435  	s->addRow(row);
 6436  }

 6438  // A blank row. Group headers butt straight against whatever precedes them,

 6450  // A label/value row: the same read-only fact shape the rest of the app
 6451  // uses (see NETWORK SETTINGS' IP ADDRESS and the system information
 6452  // page) - a label with the value as its right-hand component. Passing a
 6453  // func makes the row actionable; without one it is a plain fact.
 6454  static void cloudSetupAddFact(GuiSettings* s, Window* window, const std::string& label, const std::string& value, const std::function<void()>& func = nullptr)
 6455  {
 6456  	auto theme = ThemeData::getMenuTheme();

 6458  }
 6459  
 6460  // The sign-in pages that own a waiting `cloud_oauth serve` (#308 8a gpt
 6461  // F-ES-17). A page handed on to the next one by cloudSetupPresent leaves
 6462  // the session to it; a page that closes any other way -- EXIT, back -- is
 6463  // the player leaving the sign-in, and the session is cancelled then rather
 6464  // than left listening until its own 900-second timeout. The interface
 6465  // thread only, like the pages.
 6466  static std::set<GuiSettings*> sOAuthSessionPages;
 6467  
 6468  static void cloudOAuthOwnSession(GuiSettings* s)
 6469  {
 6470  	sOAuthSessionPages.insert(s);
 6471  	s->onFinalize([s]
 6472  	{
 6473  		if (sOAuthSessionPages.erase(s) == 0)
 6474  			return;   // handed on: the next page owns the session
 6475  		LOG(LogInfo) << "cloud_oauth: the sign-in was left; cancelling its session";
 6476  		Utils::Platform::runSystemCommand("/usr/bin/cloud_oauth cancel", "", nullptr);
 6477  	});
 6478  }
 6479  
 6480  // Show a freshly built wizard page: give it the standard large-menu
 6481  // height (the cap MenuComponent::updateSize applies) so the window does
 6482  // not jump between steps, push it, then close the page it replaces -
 6483  // closing first would flash the menu underneath.
 6484  static void cloudSetupPresent(Window* window, GuiSettings* s, GuiSettings* prev, bool completeInstructions = false)
 6485  {
 6486  	if (prev != nullptr)
 6487  		sOAuthSessionPages.erase(prev);   // handed on, not left
 6488  	if (!Renderer::ScreenSettings::fullScreenMenus())
 6489  	{
 6490  		float width = Renderer::getScreenWidth() * 0.90f;
 6491  		if (!completeInstructions && width > Renderer::getScreenHeight())
 6492  			width = (float)Renderer::getScreenHeight();
 6493  		// Instruction-only rows cannot scroll into view by cursor movement.
 6494  		// Resize AND position this page; GuiSettings otherwise keeps its
 6495  		// 15% top offset, pushing a taller page's buttons below the panel.
 6496  		s->getMenu().setSize(width, Renderer::getScreenHeight() * (completeInstructions ? 0.90f : 0.75f));
 6497  		if (completeInstructions)
 6498  			s->getMenu().setPosition((Renderer::getScreenWidth() - width) / 2,
 6499  				Renderer::getScreenHeight() * 0.025f);
 6500  	}
 6501  	window->pushGui(s);
 6502  	if (prev != nullptr)
 6503  		prev->close();
 6504  }
 6505  
 6506  // Bottom button row for a wizard page: EXIT SETUP on the left and,
 6507  // once the step's check has passed, CONTINUE on the right - replacing
 6508  // the default BACK button so each page offers exactly those two ways
 6509  // out.
 6510  static void cloudSetupSetButtons(GuiSettings* s, const std::function<void()>& onContinue)
 6511  {
 6512  	s->getMenu().clearButtons();
 6513  	// One word each. A button bar is read at a glance and the page title
 6514  	// already says what is being exited, so "EXIT SETUP" spent width on a
 6515  	// word that carried nothing -- and the wider the buttons, the more two
 6516  	// of them read as a single control.
 6517  	s->getMenu().addButton(_("EXIT"), _("exit setup"), [s] { s->close(); });
 6518  	if (onContinue != nullptr)
 6519  		s->getMenu().addButton(_("CONTINUE"), _("continue"), onContinue);
 6520  }
 6521  
 6522  // View or change the SSH password; onDone runs after the page closes.
 6523  static void cloudSetupOpenPasswordPage(Window* window, const std::string& current, const std::function<void()>& onDone)
 6524  {
 6525  	auto pw = new GuiSettings(window, _("SSH PASSWORD"));
 6526  	cloudSetupAddFact(pw, window, _("CURRENT PASSWORD"), current.empty() ? _("<NOT SET>") : current);
 6527  	pw->addInputTextConfigRow(_("CHANGE PASSWORD"), "root.password", false);
 6528  	pw->addSaveFunc([current]
 6529  	{
 6530  		// Apply only a real change; running setrootpass with an
 6531  		// empty value would clear the device password.
 6532  		const std::string changed = SystemConf::getInstance()->get("root.password");
 6533  		if (!changed.empty() && changed != current)
 6534  		{
 6535  			SystemConf::getInstance()->saveSystemConf();
 6536  			LOG(LogInfo) << "cloud_setup wizard: applying new device password";
 6537  			Utils::Platform::runSystemCommand("setrootpass " + Utils::String::shellQuote(changed), "", nullptr); // fork #198
 6538  		}
 6539  	});
 6540  	pw->onFinalize(onDone);
 6541  	window->pushGui(pw);
 6542  }
 6543  
 6544  // Edit the cloud folder (SYNCPATH) the sync tools read from and write
 6545  // to, without touching config files by hand. onDone runs after a
 6546  // successful change.
 6547  static void cloudSetupOpenPathEditor(Window* window, const std::string& title, const std::string& command,
 6548  	const std::string& current, const std::function<void()>& onDone)
 6549  {
 6550  	auto save = [window, onDone, command, current](const std::string& value)
 6551  	{
 6552  		const std::string path = Utils::String::trim(value);
 6553  		if (path == current) return;
 6554  		if (path.empty())
 6555  		{
 6556  			window->pushGui(new GuiMsgBox(window, _("ENTER A CLOUD FOLDER PATH."), _("OK")));
 6557  			return;
 6558  		}
 6559  		using PathResult = std::pair<CloudText::FolderPathRefusal, int>;
 6560  		window->pushGui(new GuiLoading<PathResult>(window, _("CHECKING..."),
 6561  			[path, command](IGuiLoadingHandler*)
 6562  			{
 6563  				auto reason = CloudText::FolderPathRefusal::Unknown;
 6564  				const auto result = ApiSystem::executeScriptLegacy("timeout 30 /usr/bin/cloud_setup " + command
 6565  					+ " " + Utils::String::shellQuote(path), [&reason](const std::string& line)
 6566  					{
 6567  						const auto known = CloudText::folderPathRefusal(line);
 6568  						if (reason == CloudText::FolderPathRefusal::Unknown)
 6569  							reason = known;
 6570  					});
 6571  				return PathResult(reason, result.second);
 6572  			},
 6573  			[window, onDone](PathResult result)
 6574  			{
 6575  				if (result.second != 0)
 6576  				{
 6577  					window->pushGui(new GuiMsgBox(window,
 6578  						_("THE CLOUD FOLDER WAS NOT CHANGED.") + "\n\n" +
 6579  						CloudText::folderPathRefusalMessage(result.first, result.second), _("OK")));
 6580  					return;
 6581  				}
 6582  				sCloudScanRun.clear();
 6583  				sCloudContentScanRun.clear();
 6584  				LOG(LogInfo) << "cloud_setup wizard: selected folder changed";
 6585  				if (onDone) onDone();
 6586  			}));
 6587  	};
 6588  	if (Settings::getInstance()->getBool("UseOSK"))
 6589  		window->pushGui(new GuiTextEditPopupKeyboard(window, title, current, save, false));
 6590  	else
 6591  		window->pushGui(new GuiTextEditPopup(window, title, current, save, false));
 6592  }
 6593  static void cloudSetupOpenSyncPathEditor(Window* window, const std::string& current, const std::function<void()>& onDone)
 6594  {
 6595  	cloudSetupOpenPathEditor(window, _("SAVES FOLDER"), "--set-syncpath", current, onDone);
 6596  }
 6597  static void cloudOpenFolderSettings(Window* window, const std::function<void()>& onDone)
 6598  {
 6599  	auto s = new GuiSettings(window, _("CLOUD FOLDERS"));
 6600  	auto info = cloudSetupInfo();
 6601  	cloudSetupAddProse(s, window, _("CHOOSE WHERE TO LOOK"),
 6602  		_("CHANGING A PATH DOES NOT MOVE YOUR FILES."));
 6603  	const std::vector<std::tuple<std::string, std::string, std::string>> folders = {
 6604  		{_("SAVES FOLDER"), "SAVES_REMOTE", "--set-syncpath"},
 6605  		{_("SETTINGS FOLDER"), "SETTINGS_REMOTE", "--set-settings-remote"},
 6606  		{_("ROMS, BIOS, AND GAME CONTENT FOLDER"), "CONTENT_REMOTE", "--set-content-remote"}
 6607  	};
 6608  	for (const auto& item : folders)
 6609  	{
 6610  		const auto title = std::get<0>(item), path = info[std::get<1>(item)], command = std::get<2>(item);
 6611  		s->addWithDescription(title, path, nullptr, [window, s, title, path, command, onDone]
 6612  		{
 6613  			const auto editorTitle = command == "--set-content-remote"
 6614  				? _("ROMS, BIOS, AND GAME CONTENT") : title;
 6615  			cloudSetupOpenPathEditor(window, editorTitle, command, path, [window, s, onDone]
 6616  			{
 6617  				cloudOpenFolderSettings(window, onDone);
 6618  				s->onFinalize(nullptr);
 6619  				s->close();
 6620  			});
 6621  		}, "", false, true);
 6622  	}
 6623  	s->onFinalize(onDone);
 6624  	window->pushGui(s);
 6625  }
 6626  
 6627  // Run `cloud_setup --check [remote]` and hand (exit code, remote name) to
 6628  // onResult on the UI thread. 0 = working, 1 = missing, 2 = unreachable.
 6629  // The only slow call in the wizard - a remote check can take up to the
 6630  // rclone timeout - so the only one behind the GuiLoading spinner.
 6631  static void cloudSetupRunCheck(Window* window, const std::string& remote, const std::function<void(int, const std::string&)>& onResult)
 6632  {
 6633  	std::string cmd = "/usr/bin/cloud_setup --check";
 6634  	if (!remote.empty())
 6635  		cmd += " " + Utils::String::shellQuote(remote);
 6636  	window->pushGui(new GuiLoading<std::pair<int, std::string>>(window, _("CHECKING..."),
 6637  		[cmd](auto gui)
 6638  		{
 6639  			std::string out = Utils::String::trim(Utils::Platform::GetShOutput(cmd + "; echo RC=$?"));
 6640  			int rc = 1;
 6641  			auto pos = out.rfind("RC=");
 6642  			if (pos != std::string::npos)
 6643  			{
 6644  				rc = atoi(out.substr(pos + 3).c_str());
 6645  				out = Utils::String::trim(out.substr(0, pos));
 6646  			}
 6647  			std::string name = out.find(' ') != std::string::npos ? out.substr(out.find(' ') + 1) : "";
 6648  			LOG(LogInfo) << "cloud_setup wizard: --check rc=" << rc << " remote=" << name;
 6649  			return std::pair<int, std::string>(rc, name);
 6650  		},
 6651  		[onResult](std::pair<int, std::string> result)
 6652  		{
 6653  			onResult(result.first, result.second);
 6654  		}));
 6655  }
 6656  
 6657  // Step 1 of 3: SSH must be reachable - a device password and a running
 6658  // SSH service, checked together on one page. Each actionable row is
 6659  // marked with its state; CONTINUE appears once both checks pass.
 6660  static void cloudSetupShowSshStep(Window* window, CloudSetupMode mode, const std::string& remote, const std::string& preexisting, GuiSettings* prev)
 6661  {
 6662  	auto info = cloudSetupInfo();
 6663  	if (info["IP"].empty())
 6664  	{
 6665  		window->pushGui(new GuiMsgBox(window, _("NO NETWORK CONNECTION DETECTED.\n\nCONNECT TO A NETWORK FIRST, THEN TRY AGAIN.")));
 6666  		return;
 6667  	}
 6668  
 6669  	// cloud_setup --info says whether a password is set, never what it is:
 6670  	// a credential that crosses a script boundary is one careless echo from
 6671  	// a log (#116, D-INFRA-010). The value, where this page must show or
 6672  	// pre-fill it, is read in-process from the device's own settings.
 6673  	const bool pwOk = info["PASSWORD_SET"] == "1";
 6674  	const bool sshOk = info["SSH_UP"] == "active";
 6675  	LOG(LogInfo) << "cloud_setup wizard: step 1 ssh setup, password=*** << pwOk << " sshd=" << info["SSH_UP"];
 6676  
 6677  	auto s = new GuiSettings(window, cloudSetupTitle(mode));
 6678  	s->setSubTitle(_("STEP 1 OF 3 - SET UP SSH"));
 6679  
 6680  	const std::string current = SystemConf::getInstance()->get("root.password");
 6681  	s->addEntry((pwOk ? _U("\uF058  ") : _U("\uF071  ")) + _("SET SSH PASSWORD"), true, [window, s, mode, remote, preexisting, current]
 6682  	{
 6683  		cloudSetupOpenPasswordPage(window, current, [window, s, mode, remote, preexisting]
 6684  		{
 6685  			cloudSetupShowSshStep(window, mode, remote, preexisting, s);
 6686  		});
 6687  	});
 6688  
 6689  	if (sshOk)
 6690  		cloudSetupAddInfoRow(s, window, _U("\uF058  ") + _("SSH SERVICE IS ENABLED"));
 6691  	else
 6692  		s->addEntry(_U("\uF071  ") + _("ENABLE SSH SERVICE"), true, [window, s, mode, remote, preexisting]
 6693  		{
 6694  			LOG(LogInfo) << "cloud_setup wizard: enabling sshd";
 6695  			Utils::Platform::runSystemCommand("mkdir -p /storage/.cache/services/", "", nullptr);
 6696  			Utils::Platform::runSystemCommand("touch /storage/.cache/services/sshd.conf", "", nullptr);
 6697  			Utils::Platform::runSystemCommand("systemctl enable sshd", "", nullptr);
 6698  			Utils::Platform::runSystemCommand("systemctl start sshd", "", nullptr);
 6699  			SystemConf::getInstance()->set("ssh.enabled", "1");
 6700  			SystemConf::getInstance()->saveSystemConf();
 6701  			cloudSetupShowSshStep(window, mode, remote, preexisting, s);
 6702  		});
 6703  
 6704  	std::function<void()> onContinue = nullptr;
 6705  	if (pwOk && sshOk)
 6706  		onContinue = [window, s, mode, remote, preexisting]
 6707  		{
 6708  			cloudSetupShowConnectStep(window, mode, remote, preexisting, s);
 6709  		};
 6710  	cloudSetupSetButtons(s, onContinue);
 6711  
 6712  	cloudSetupPresent(window, s, prev);
 6713  }
 6714  
 6715  // Step 2 of 3: connect from the computer; CONTINUE passes only when
 6716  // --connected sees an established inbound SSH session.
 6717  static void cloudSetupShowConnectStep(Window* window, CloudSetupMode mode, const std::string& remote, const std::string& preexisting, GuiSettings* prev)
 6718  {
 6719  	auto info = cloudSetupInfo();
 6720  	LOG(LogInfo) << "cloud_setup wizard: step 2 connect, override=" << info["OVERRIDE"];
 6721  
 6722  	auto s = new GuiSettings(window, cloudSetupTitle(mode));
 6723  	s->setSubTitle(_("STEP 2 OF 3 - CONNECT FROM YOUR COMPUTER"));
 6724  
 6725  	s->addGroup(_("HOW TO CONNECT"));
 6726  	cloudSetupAddProse(s, window, _("ON YOUR COMPUTER"),
 6727  		_("OPEN A TERMINAL ON A COMPUTER ON THE SAME NETWORK, RUN THE COMMAND BELOW, AND ENTER THE PASSWORD WHEN ASKED."));
 6728  	cloudSetupAddInfoRow(s, window, info["SSH_CMD"], true);
 6729  
 6730  	cloudSetupAddSpacer(s, window);
 6731  	s->addGroup(_("CONNECTION DETAILS"));
 6732  	// Read in-process, not from the script (#116): this row exists so the
 6733  	// player can type the password into their computer's ssh prompt.
 6734  	const std::string current = SystemConf::getInstance()->get("root.password");
 6735  	cloudSetupAddFact(s, window, _("CURRENT PASSWORD"), current, [window, s, mode, remote, preexisting, current]
 6736  	{
 6737  		cloudSetupOpenPasswordPage(window, current, [window, s, mode, remote, preexisting]
 6738  		{
 6739  			cloudSetupShowConnectStep(window, mode, remote, preexisting, s);
 6740  		});
 6741  	});
 6742  	// With a connection override the direct address is not the one to
 6743  	// use, so showing it would only mislead.
 6744  	if (info["OVERRIDE"] != "1")
 6745  		cloudSetupAddFact(s, window, _("DEVICE IP"), info["IP"]);
 6746  
 6747  	cloudSetupSetButtons(s, [window, s, mode, remote, preexisting]
 6748  	{
 6749  		bool connected = Utils::String::trim(Utils::Platform::GetShOutput("/usr/bin/cloud_setup --connected")) == "CONNECTED";
 6750  		LOG(LogInfo) << "cloud_setup wizard: --connected=" << connected;
 6751  		if (connected)
 6752  		{
 6753  			cloudSetupShowConfigureStep(window, mode, remote, preexisting, s);
 6754  			return;
 6755  		}
 6756  		window->pushGui(new GuiMsgBox(window,
 6757  			_("THERE IS NO ACTIVE SSH CONNECTION TO THE DEVICE.\n\nTRY CONNECTING FROM YOUR COMPUTER AGAIN, LEAVE THE TERMINAL OPEN, AND THEN SELECT 'CONTINUE'."),
 6758  			_("OK"), nullptr,
 6759  			_("EXIT"), [s] { s->close(); }));
 6760  	});
 6761  
 6762  	cloudSetupPresent(window, s, prev);
 6763  }
 6764  
 6765  // Gate for the configure step: advance to the done step only when the
 6766  // checked remote verifies; otherwise explain and stay (or exit).
 6767  static void cloudSetupGateCheck(Window* window, GuiSettings* s, const std::string& remote)
 6768  {
 6769  	cloudSetupRunCheck(window, remote, [window, s](int rc, const std::string& name)
 6770  	{
 6771  		if (rc == 0)
 6772  		{
 6773  			cloudSetupShowDoneStep(window, name, s);
 6774  		}
 6775  		else if (rc == 2)
 6776  			window->pushGui(new GuiMsgBox(window,
 6777  				_("THIS CONNECTION ISN'T ANSWERING:") + " " + cloudSetupDisplayName(name) + "\n\n" + _("ITS SIGN-IN MAY BE INCOMPLETE OR EXPIRED. IN THE TERMINAL, RUN:") + "\n'rclone config reconnect " + name + "'",
 6778  				_("OK"), nullptr,
 6779  				_("EXIT"), [s] { s->close(); }));
 6780  		else
 6781  			window->pushGui(new GuiMsgBox(window,
 6782  				_("NO CLOUD STORAGE IS SET UP ON THIS DEVICE YET.\n\nFINISH 'RCLONE CONFIG' IN THE TERMINAL, THEN SELECT 'CONTINUE' AGAIN."),
 6783  				_("OK"), nullptr,
 6784  				_("EXIT"), [s] { s->close(); }));
 6785  	});
 6786  }
 6787  
 6788  // Step 3 of 3: what to do inside the SSH session, as short numbered
 6789  // steps branched by scenario; CONTINUE passes only when the remote
 6790  // verifies via --check. The auto-config sign-in must go through the
 6791  // step 2 tunnel - `rclone authorize` on the computer cannot bind its
 6792  // port while that tunnel is open, so the instructions forbid it, and a
 6793  // stale sign-in webserver on the device gets a clear-it action.
 6794  static void cloudSetupShowConfigureStep(Window* window, CloudSetupMode mode, const std::string& remote, const std::string& preexisting, GuiSettings* prev)
 6795  {
 6796  	auto info = cloudSetupInfo();
 6797  	LOG(LogInfo) << "cloud_setup wizard: step 3 configure, mode=" << (int)mode << " remote=" << remote << " auth_port=" << info["AUTH_PORT"];
 6798  
 6799  	auto s = new GuiSettings(window, cloudSetupTitle(mode));
 6800  
 6801  	if (mode == CloudSetupMode::RepairRemote)
 6802  	{
 6803  		s->setSubTitle(_("STEP 3 OF 3 - REPAIR THE CONNECTION"));
 6804  		s->addGroup(_("IN THE TERMINAL"));
 6805  		cloudSetupAddInfoRow(s, window, _("1. RUN 'rclone config reconnect ") + remote + "'");
 6806  		cloudSetupAddInfoRow(s, window, _("2. THAT RENEWS AN EXPIRED SIGN-IN."));
 6807  		cloudSetupAddInfoRow(s, window, _("3. FOR OTHER OPTIONS: RUN 'rclone config', PRESS 'E' TO EDIT."));
 6808  		cloudSetupAddInfoRow(s, window, _("4. PRESS 'Q' TO QUIT WHEN YOU ARE DONE."));
 6809  	}
 6810  	else
 6811  	{
 6812  		s->setSubTitle(_("STEP 3 OF 3 - CREATE THE CONNECTION"));
 6813  		s->addGroup(_("IN THE TERMINAL"));
 6814  		// Step 5 is the one people get wrong: rclone's own prompt says to
 6815  		// answer N on a machine with no browser, which describes this
 6816  		// handheld exactly. Answering N is fatal here.
 6817  		// Steps 6-7 describe what rclone actually does. There is no browser
 6818  		// on the device, so it always takes its "Failed to open browser
 6819  		// automatically ... please go to the following link" branch and
 6820  		// prints the URL to the terminal. Saying "the link in your browser"
 6821  		// implied one opens by itself and left people with nothing to click.
 6822  		cloudSetupAddInfoRow(s, window, _("1. RUN 'rclone config'"));
 6823  		cloudSetupAddInfoRow(s, window, _("2. PRESS 'N' FOR A NEW REMOTE (RCLONE'S WORD FOR A CONNECTION)"));
 6824  		cloudSetupAddInfoRow(s, window, _("3. NAME IT, PICK YOUR PROVIDER, TAKE DEFAULTS"));
 6825  		cloudSetupAddInfoRow(s, window, _("4. AT 'USE AUTO CONFIG?' PRESS 'Y'"));
 6826  		cloudSetupAddInfoRow(s, window, _("5. 'FAILED TO OPEN BROWSER' IS NORMAL HERE"));
 6827  		cloudSetupAddInfoRow(s, window, _("6. COPY THE LINK IT PRINTS INTO YOUR BROWSER"));
 6828  		cloudSetupAddInfoRow(s, window, _("7. SIGN IN, THEN PRESS 'Q' WHEN THE REMOTE IS LISTED"));
 6829  		// Kept short on purpose: this page has no selectable rows, so
 6830  		// ComponentList cannot scroll it (the camera follows the cursor,
 6831  		// and the cursor skips unselectable rows). Anything past the fold
 6832  		// is unreachable, so the text has to fit. Steps 5 and 6 already
 6833  		// say to answer 'Y' and sign in; the only new fact is the
 6834  		// 'rclone authorize' trap.
 6835  		s->addGroup(_("IMPORTANT"));
 6836  		cloudSetupAddProse(s, window, _("DO NOT USE 'rclone authorize'"),
 6837  			_("IT CANNOT BIND ITS PORT WHILE STEP 2'S CONNECTION IS OPEN."));
 6838  		if (mode == CloudSetupMode::AddRemote)
 6839  			cloudSetupAddInfoRow(s, window, _("CHOOSE WHICH CONNECTION TO USE UNDER CLOUD CONNECTIONS."));
 6840  	}
 6841  
 6842  	if (info["AUTH_PORT"] == "busy")
 6843  		s->addEntry(_U("\uF071  ") + _("A STALE SIGN-IN IS BLOCKING THE PORT - PRESS TO CLEAR IT"), true, [window, s, mode, remote, preexisting]
 6844  		{
 6845  			LOG(LogInfo) << "cloud_setup wizard: freeing stale auth port";
 6846  			Utils::Platform::runSystemCommand("/usr/bin/cloud_setup --free-auth-port", "", nullptr);
 6847  			cloudSetupShowConfigureStep(window, mode, remote, preexisting, s);
 6848  		});
 6849  
 6850  	cloudSetupSetButtons(s, [window, s, mode, remote, preexisting]
 6851  	{
 6852  		if (mode == CloudSetupMode::AddRemote)
 6853  		{
 6854  			// The new remote is whichever name was not configured when the
 6855  			// wizard started - gate on that one, not the existing first.
 6856  			std::string newRemote;
 6857  			for (auto& name : Utils::String::split(cloudSetupInfo()["REMOTES"], ' '))
 6858  			{
 6859  				auto n = Utils::String::trim(name);
 6860  				if (!n.empty() && (" " + preexisting + " ").find(" " + n + " ") == std::string::npos)
 6861  					newRemote = n;
 6862  			}
 6863  			LOG(LogInfo) << "cloud_setup wizard: add-mode new remote=" << newRemote;
 6864  			if (newRemote.empty())
 6865  			{
 6866  				window->pushGui(new GuiMsgBox(window,
 6867  					_("NO NEW CONNECTION WAS ADDED YET.\n\nFINISH 'RCLONE CONFIG' IN THE TERMINAL, THEN SELECT 'CONTINUE' AGAIN."),
 6868  					_("OK"), nullptr,
 6869  					_("EXIT"), [s] { s->close(); }));
 6870  				return;
 6871  			}
 6872  			cloudSetupGateCheck(window, s, newRemote);
 6873  			return;
 6874  		}
 6875  		cloudSetupGateCheck(window, s, mode == CloudSetupMode::RepairRemote ? remote : "");
 6876  	});
 6877  
 6878  	cloudSetupPresent(window, s, prev, true);
 6879  }
 6880  
 6881  // Folder checks are explicit, selected-category reads. Linking alone does not
 6882  // create anything or look outside the selected roots.
 6883  static std::string cloudFolderCategoryLabel(const std::string& category)
 6884  {
 6885  	if (category == "saves") return _("SAVES");
 6886  	if (category == "settings") return _("SETTINGS");
 6887  	if (category == "roms") return _("ROMS");
 6888  	if (category == "bios") return _("BIOS FILES");
 6889  	return _("GAME CONTENT");
 6890  }
 6891  static std::string cloudFolderStateLabel(const std::string& state)
 6892  {
 6893  	if (state == "present") return _("FILES FOUND");
 6894  	if (state == "missing") return _("FOLDER MISSING");
 6895  	if (state == "empty") return _("NO FILES FOUND");
 6896  	if (state == "misplaced") return _("CHECK FILE LOCATIONS");
 6897  	return _("COULDN'T READ THIS FOLDER");
 6898  }
 6899  // Legacy line readers discard chunks longer than their line buffer. JSON is
 6900  // a byte stream, and a populated five-category result can exceed that buffer.
 6901  static std::pair<std::string, int> cloudReadFolderJson(const std::string& command)
 6902  {
 6903  	FILE* pipe = popen(command.c_str(), "r");
 6904  	if (!pipe) return {"", -1};
 6905  	std::string json;
 6906  	char buffer[1024];
 6907  	bool bounded = true;
 6908  	std::size_t count;
 6909  	while ((count = fread(buffer, 1, sizeof(buffer), pipe)) != 0)
 6910  		if (bounded) bounded = CloudFolderValidation::appendOutput(json, buffer, count);
 6911  	const bool failed = ferror(pipe) != 0;
 6912  	const int status = pclose(pipe);
 6913  	if (!bounded || failed) json.clear();
 6914  	return {json, status != -1 && WIFEXITED(status) ? WEXITSTATUS(status) : -1};
 6915  }
 6916  static CloudFolderValidation::Context cloudFolderContext()
 6917  {
 6918  	const auto output = cloudReadFolderJson("timeout 5 /usr/bin/cloud_setup --validation-context");
 6919  	return output.second == 0 ? CloudFolderValidation::parseContext(output.first) : CloudFolderValidation::Context();
 6920  }
 6921  static void cloudFolderInstructions(Window* window, const CloudFolderValidation::Category& item)
 6922  {
 6923  	auto s = new GuiSettings(window, _("CLOUD FOLDER INSTRUCTIONS"));
 6924  	s->addGroup(cloudFolderCategoryLabel(item.category));
 6925  	cloudSetupAddProse(s, window, _("CLOUD FOLDER"), item.path);
 6926  	if (item.category == "roms")
 6927  		cloudSetupAddProse(s, window, _("ADD ROMS FROM A COMPUTER"),
 6928  			_("PUT EACH SYSTEM'S ROMS IN ITS OWN FOLDER HERE, SUCH AS nes OR gba. THEN CHECK AGAIN."));
 6929  	else if (item.category == "bios")
 6930  		cloudSetupAddProse(s, window, _("ADD BIOS FILES FROM A COMPUTER"),
 6931  			_("PUT YOUR BIOS FILES HERE, KEEPING THE NAMES AND SUBFOLDERS REQUIRED BY YOUR EMULATORS."));
 6932  	else if (item.category == "media")
 6933  		cloudSetupAddProse(s, window, _("ADD GAME CONTENT FROM A COMPUTER"),
 6934  			_("PUT EACH SYSTEM'S ARTWORK, VIDEOS, MANUALS, AND GAME LIST HERE IN ITS SYSTEM FOLDER."));
 6935  	else if (item.category == "saves")
 6936  		cloudSetupAddProse(s, window, _("ADD SAVES"),
 6937  			_("BACK UP GAME SAVES, SAVE STATES, AND SCREENSHOTS TO THIS FOLDER, OR COPY THEM HERE FROM A COMPUTER."));
 6938  	else
 6939  		cloudSetupAddProse(s, window, _("SETTINGS BACKUPS"),
 6940  			_("BACK UP SETTINGS TO POPULATE THIS FOLDER, OR COPY A SETTINGS BACKUP HERE FROM A COMPUTER."));
 6941  	cloudSetupAddProse(s, window, _("AFTER ADDING FILES"),
 6942  		_("CHECK FOLDERS AGAIN. THIS CHECK DOES NOT TEST FILE INTEGRITY OR GAME COMPATIBILITY."));
 6943  	window->pushGui(s);
 6944  }
 6945  static void cloudShowFolderResult(Window* window, const CloudFolderValidation::Result& result)
 6946  {
 6947  	if (!result.valid)
 6948  	{
 6949  		window->pushGui(new GuiMsgBox(window,
 6950  			_("COULDN'T CHECK YOUR FOLDERS. CHECK YOUR CONNECTION, THEN TRY AGAIN."), _("OK")));
 6951  		return;
 6952  	}
 6953  	auto s = new GuiSettings(window, _("CLOUD FOLDER CHECK"));
 6954  	s->setSubTitle(result.complete ? _("COMPLETED") : _("COULDN'T FINISH"));
 6955  	cloudSetupAddProse(s, window, _("FOLDER LOCATIONS ONLY"),
 6956  		_("FILES FOUND DOES NOT MEAN THEY ARE COMPLETE OR COMPATIBLE."));
 6957  	for (const auto& item : result.categories)
 6958  	{
 6959  		s->addGroup(cloudFolderCategoryLabel(item.category));
 6960  		cloudSetupAddProse(s, window, cloudFolderStateLabel(item.state), item.path);
 6961  		s->addEntry(_("SEE INSTRUCTIONS"), true, [window, item] { cloudFolderInstructions(window, item); });
 6962  	}
 6963  	window->pushGui(s);
 6964  }
 6965  static void cloudCheckFolders(Window* window, const std::vector<std::string>& selected)
 6966  {
 6967  	if (selected.empty())
 6968  	{
 6969  		window->pushGui(new GuiMsgBox(window, _("CHOOSE AT LEAST ONE ITEM TO CHECK."), _("OK")));
 6970  		return;
 6971  	}
 6972  	const std::string run = cloudNewCheckId();
 6973  	std::string csv;
 6974  	for (const auto& category : selected) csv += (csv.empty() ? "" : ",") + category;
 6975  	LOG(LogInfo) << "cloud_setup wizard: checking selected folders, run=" << run;
 6976  	window->pushGui(new GuiLoading<CloudFolderValidation::Result>(window, _("CHECKING YOUR CLOUD FOLDERS"),
 6977  		[selected, csv, run](IGuiLoadingHandler*)
 6978  	{
 6979  			const auto started = cloudFolderContext();
 6980  			if (!started.valid) return CloudFolderValidation::Result();
 6981  			const auto output = cloudReadFolderJson("timeout 35 /usr/bin/cloud_setup --validate-folders "
 6982  				+ Utils::String::shellQuote(csv) + " " + Utils::String::shellQuote(run));
 6983  			auto result = CloudFolderValidation::parseResult(output.first, run, started, cloudFolderContext(), selected);
 6984  			if (output.second != 0) result.complete = false;
 6985  			return result;
 6986  		}, [window](CloudFolderValidation::Result result) { cloudShowFolderResult(window, result); }));
 6987  }
 6988  static void cloudShowFolderSelection(Window* window, GuiSettings* prev, const std::vector<std::string>& selected)
 6989  {
 6990  	auto s = new GuiSettings(window, _("CLOUD FOLDERS"));
 6991  	cloudSetupAddProse(s, window, _("CHOOSE FOLDERS"),
 6992  		_("SELECT WHAT TO CHECK OR CREATE FOLDERS FOR. THIS DOES NOT CHANGE AUTOMATIC SYNC."));
 6993  	const auto context = cloudFolderContext();
 6994  	auto switches = std::make_shared<std::vector<std::pair<std::string, std::shared_ptr<SwitchComponent>>>>();
 6995  	for (const auto& category : std::vector<std::string>{"saves", "settings", "roms", "bios", "media"})
 6996  	{
 6997  		auto toggle = std::make_shared<SwitchComponent>(window);
 6998  		toggle->setState(std::find(selected.begin(), selected.end(), category) != selected.end());
 6999  		switches->push_back({category, toggle});
 7000  		const auto path = context.paths.find(category);
 7001  		s->addWithDescription(cloudFolderCategoryLabel(category),
 7002  			path == context.paths.end() ? _("<NOT SET>") : path->second, toggle);
 7003  	}
 7004  	auto picked = [switches]
 7005  	{
 7006  		std::vector<std::string> selected;
 7007  		for (const auto& item : *switches) if (item.second->getState()) selected.push_back(item.first);
 7008  		return selected;
 7009  	};
 7010  	// A page describes the paths an action will use. Even an external settings
 7011  	// change must not turn a press on an old path into work somewhere else.
 7012  	auto stillSelected = [window, context]
 7013  	{
 7014  		const auto current = cloudFolderContext();
 7015  		if (context.valid && current.valid && context.configId == current.configId && context.paths == current.paths)
 7016  			return true;
 7017  		cloudCheckChanged(window);
 7018  		return false;
 7019  	};
 7020  	s->addGroup(_("FOLDER ACTIONS"));
 7021  	s->addEntry(_("CHECK FOLDERS"), true, [window, picked, stillSelected]
 7022  	{
 7023  		if (stillSelected()) cloudCheckFolders(window, picked());
 7024  	});
 7025  	s->addEntry(_("CREATE FOLDERS"), true, [window, picked, stillSelected]
 7026  	{
 7027  		if (!stillSelected()) return;
 7028  		const auto selected = picked();
 7029  		if (selected.empty())
 7030  		{
 7031  			window->pushGui(new GuiMsgBox(window, _("CHOOSE AT LEAST ONE ITEM TO CREATE FOLDERS FOR."), _("OK")));
 7032  			return;
 7033  		}
 7034  		std::string csv;
 7035  		for (const auto& category : selected) csv += (csv.empty() ? "" : ",") + category;
 7036  		window->pushGui(new GuiMsgBox(window,
 7037  			_("CREATE FOLDERS AND SETUP NOTES FOR THE ITEMS YOU CHOSE?\n\nYOUR FILES WILL NOT BE MOVED OR REORGANIZED."),
 7038  			_("YES"), [window, csv, selected, stillSelected]
 7039  			{
 7040  				if (!stillSelected()) return;
 7041  				auto page = new GuiCloudTransfer(window, "/usr/bin/cloud_setup --seed-folders "
 7042  					+ Utils::String::shellQuote(csv), _("CREATING CLOUD FOLDERS"), (int)selected.size());
 7043  				page->setAutoContinue([window, selected] { cloudCheckFolders(window, selected); });
 7044  				window->pushGui(page);
 7045  			}, _("NO"), nullptr));
 7046  	});
 7047  	s->addEntry(_("CHANGE CLOUD FOLDER"), true, [window, s, picked]
 7048  	{
 7049  		cloudOpenFolderSettings(window, [window, s, picked]
 7050  		{
 7051  			cloudShowFolderSelection(window, s, picked());
 7052  		});
 7053  	});
 7054  	s->getMenu().clearButtons();
 7055  	s->getMenu().addButton(_("FINISH"), _("finish"), [s] { s->close(); });
 7056  	cloudSetupPresent(window, s, prev);
 7057  }
 7058  static void cloudSetupShowDoneStep(Window* window, const std::string& remote, GuiSettings* prev)
 7059  {
 7060  	const auto active = Utils::String::trim(Utils::Platform::GetShOutput("/usr/bin/cloud_remote active"));
 7061  	if (!remote.empty() && remote + (remote.back() == ':' ? "" : ":") != active)
 7062  	{
 7063  		auto s = new GuiSettings(window, _("CONNECTION SAVED"));
 7064  		cloudSetupAddProse(s, window, remote,
 7065  			_("YOUR CURRENT CONNECTION HAS NOT CHANGED. CHOOSE THIS CONNECTION UNDER CLOUD CONNECTIONS WHEN YOU WANT TO USE IT."));
 7066  		cloudSetupPresent(window, s, prev);
 7067  		return;
 7068  	}
 7069  	cloudShowFolderSelection(window, prev, {"saves", "settings"});
 7070  }
 7071  
 7072  
 7073  // Wizard entry point: network is a hard precondition; then branch on the
 7074  // scenario - no remotes yet goes straight into the first-remote wizard,
 7075  // otherwise offer check / add / repair-or-modify for the existing ones.
 7076  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
 7077  // Add a cloud remote without rclone's interactive configuration.
 7078  //
```
