```text
 7117  // Alibaba, ArvanCloud..." is accurate and useless on a handheld -- it is
 7118  // truncated mid-word in a menu and tells the player nothing the first three
 7119  // characters did not.
 7120  // The table and the label it gives a provider live in CloudText, which is
 7121  // where the pure cloud text is tested from (es-app/tests/unit): translated
 7122  // at the point of use, since _() at static-init time would run before the
 7123  // locale is loaded.
 7124  
 7125  static std::vector<std::string> cloudRemoteLines(const std::string& args)
 7126  {
 7127  	// GetShOutputLines, not GetShOutput: the latter drops the last character
 7128  	// of every chunk it reads, so multi-line output arrives as one
 7129  	// concatenated string. Parsing that yields a single nonsense record
 7130  	// rather than an error, which is how this first shipped -- the provider
 7131  	// list rendered empty with nothing logged.
 7132  	std::vector<std::string> lines;
 7133  	for (auto& line : Utils::Platform::GetShOutputLines("/usr/bin/cloud_remote " + args))
 7134  		if (!Utils::String::trim(line).empty())
 7135  			lines.push_back(line);
 7136  	return lines;
 7137  }
 7138  
 7139  static std::string cloudConnectionError(const std::string& output)
 7140  {
 7141  	if (output.find("A cloud operation is running.") != std::string::npos)
 7142  		return _("A CLOUD OPERATION IS RUNNING. WAIT FOR IT TO FINISH, THEN TRY AGAIN.");
 7143  	if (output.find("Turn off both automatic") != std::string::npos)
 7144  		return _("TURN OFF BOTH AUTOMATIC SAVE SYNC OPTIONS UNDER MANAGE CLOUD STORAGE BEFORE CHANGING THIS CONNECTION.");
 7145  	if (output.find("The selected connection is missing.") != std::string::npos)
 7146  		return _("THE SELECTED CONNECTION IS MISSING. CHOOSE ANOTHER UNDER CLOUD CONNECTIONS.");
 7147  	if (output.find("different connection name") != std::string::npos)
 7148  		return _("CHOOSE A DIFFERENT CONNECTION NAME.");
 7149  	if (output.find("Another connection uses") != std::string::npos)
 7150  		return _("ANOTHER CONNECTION USES THIS ONE. CHANGE THAT CONNECTION FIRST.");
 7151  	if (output.find("additional setup") != std::string::npos)
 7152  		return _("THIS PROVIDER NEEDS ADDITIONAL SETUP. USE A COMPUTER TO FINISH CONNECTING IT.");
 7153  	if (output.find("could not be verified") != std::string::npos || output.find("stopped answering") != std::string::npos)
 7154  		return _("THE CONNECTION DIDN'T ANSWER. CHECK ITS DETAILS AND YOUR NETWORK, THEN TRY AGAIN. YOUR SETTINGS HAVE NOT CHANGED.");
 7155  	return _("YOUR CLOUD SETTINGS COULD NOT BE READ OR SAVED. TRY AGAIN.");
 7156  }

 7158  // Split a TSV record, keeping empty fields. removeEmptyEntries would drop
 7159  // them, and most rows have an empty column somewhere -- a backend option with
 7160  // no default, or no help text -- which shifts every later field left and
 7161  // silently discards the row for being too short.
 7162  static std::string cloudTsvField(const std::vector<std::string>& cols, size_t i)
 7163  {
 7164  	return i < cols.size() ? cols[i] : std::string();
 7165  }
 7166  
 7167  static std::vector<CloudBackend> cloudRemoteBackends()
 7168  {
 7169  	std::vector<CloudBackend> list;
 7170  	for (auto& line : cloudRemoteLines("providers"))
 7171  	{
 7172  		auto cols = Utils::String::splitAny(line, "\t", false);
 7173  		if (cols.size() < 3)
 7174  			continue;
 7175  		list.push_back({ cols[0], cols[1], cols[2], std::string() });
 7176  	}
 7177  	return list;
 7178  }

 7180  // Shell-quote a value on its way to cloud_remote. Passwords and keys routinely
 7181  // contain characters the shell would otherwise act on, and a mangled secret
 7182  // fails in a way that looks like the provider rejecting the credential.
 7183  static std::string cloudShellQuote(const std::string& value)
 7184  {
 7185  	std::string quoted = "'";
 7186  	for (char c : value)
 7187  	{
 7188  		if (c == '\'')
 7189  			quoted += "'\\''";
 7190  		else
 7191  			quoted += c;
 7192  	}
 7193  	return quoted + "'";
 7194  }
 7195  
 7196  // Open an editor that reads the value from `values` rather than the copy
 7197  // addInputTextRow captured when the row was built. Without this the second
 7198  // edit of a field starts from the original value again.
 7199  //
 7200  // The obvious alternative -- rebuild the page after each edit -- is unsafe
 7201  // here: GuiSettings::close() is `delete this`, and the callback doing the
 7202  // rebuild is owned by the page being deleted.
 7203  static std::function<void(Window*, std::string, std::string,
 7204  	const std::function<void(std::string)>&)>
 7205  cloudRemoteEditor(std::shared_ptr<std::map<std::string, std::string>> values,
 7206  	const std::string& key)
 7207  {
 7208  	return [values, key](Window* w, std::string title, std::string /*stale*/,
 7209  		const std::function<void(std::string)>& onsave)
 7210  	{
 7211  		std::string current = values->count(key) ? (*values)[key] : "";
 7212  		if (Settings::getInstance()->getBool("UseOSK"))
 7213  			w->pushGui(new GuiTextEditPopupKeyboard(w, title, current, onsave, false));
 7214  		else
 7215  			w->pushGui(new GuiTextEditPopup(w, title, current, onsave, false));
 7216  	};
 7217  }

 7219  // One provider's questions.
 7220  static void cloudRemoteShowForm(Window* window, const CloudBackend& backend,
 7221  	std::shared_ptr<std::map<std::string, std::string>> values, GuiSettings* prev)
 7222  {
 7223  	auto s = new GuiSettings(window, _("CONNECT CLOUD STORAGE"));
 7224  	s->setSubTitle(CloudText::providerSubtitle(backend.name, backend.label));
 7225  
 7226  	std::vector<CloudBackendField> fields;
 7227  	// The backend id is quoted: rclone has ids with spaces in them
 7228  	// ("google cloud storage", "google photos"), which would otherwise split
 7229  	// into extra arguments.
 7230  	std::string fieldsArgs = "fields " + cloudShellQuote(backend.name);
 7231  	if (!backend.subprovider.empty())
 7232  		fieldsArgs += " " + cloudShellQuote(backend.subprovider);
 7233  	for (auto& line : cloudRemoteLines(fieldsArgs))
 7234  	{
 7235  		auto cols = Utils::String::splitAny(line, "\t", false);
 7236  		if (cols.size() < 5 || cols[0].empty())
 7237  			continue;
 7238  		CloudBackendField f;
 7239  		f.name      = cloudTsvField(cols, 0);
 7240  		f.required  = cloudTsvField(cols, 1) == "1";
 7241  		f.password  = cloudTsvField(cols, 2) == "1";
 7242  		f.sensitive = cloudTsvField(cols, 3) == "1";
 7243  		f.type      = cloudTsvField(cols, 4);
 7244  		f.deflt     = cloudTsvField(cols, 5);
 7245  		f.help      = cloudTsvField(cols, 6);
 7246  		f.choices   = atoi(cloudTsvField(cols, 7).c_str());
 7247  		fields.push_back(f);
 7248  	}
 7249  
 7250  	if (values->find("__name__") == values->end())
 7251  		(*values)["__name__"] = backend.name;
 7252  
 7253  	s->addGroup(_("NAME"));
 7254  	if (values->count("__edit__"))
 7255  	{
 7256  		cloudSetupAddFact(s, window, _("CONNECTION NAME"), (*values)["__name__"]);
 7257  		cloudSetupAddProse(s, window, _("EDIT CONNECTION"),
 7258  			_("BLANK FIELDS KEEP THEIR CURRENT VALUES."));
 7259  	}
 7260  	else s->addInputTextRow(_("CONNECTION NAME"), (*values)["__name__"], false,
 7261  		cloudRemoteEditor(values, "__name__"),
 7262  		[values](const std::string& newVal) { (*values)["__name__"] = newVal; });
 7263  
 7264  	// Required first: on a small panel the fold is close, and a required field
 7265  	// below it reads as absent rather than as further down.
 7266  	for (int pass = 0; pass < 2; pass++)
 7267  	{
 7268  		bool wantRequired = (pass == 0);
 7269  		bool headed = false;
 7270  		for (auto& f : fields)
 7271  		{
 7272  			if (f.required != wantRequired)
 7273  				continue;
 7274  			if (!headed)
 7275  			{
 7276  				s->addGroup(wantRequired ? _("REQUIRED") : _("OPTIONAL"));
 7277  				headed = true;
 7278  			}
 7279  			// Mask anything the backend calls a password or marks sensitive.
 7280  			// rclone's two flags do not agree on what a credential is -- it
 7281  			// marks sftp's host sensitive, and s3's secret_access_key
 7282  			// sensitive but not a password -- so masking either flag is the
 7283  			// only rule that never leaves a key on screen. Opening the row
 7284  			// still shows the value, which is the reveal.
 7285  			bool mask = f.password || f.sensitive;
 7286  			if (!values->count("__edit__") && !values->count(f.name) && !f.deflt.empty())
 7287  				(*values)[f.name] = f.deflt;
 7288  			std::string current = values->count(f.name) ? (*values)[f.name] : "";
 7289  			// The rclone name is the key the value is stored under; the row
 7290  			// shows CloudText::fieldLabel's words for it (#123).
 7291  			std::string fieldName = f.name;
 7292  
 7293  			// Match the widget to the option. Everything was a text box before,
 7294  			// which meant typing "true" for a switch, and guessing that the
 7295  			// answer for a generic WebDAV server is the literal string "other".
 7296  			if (f.type == "bool" && !values->count("__edit__"))
 7297  			{
 7298  				auto sw = std::make_shared<SwitchComponent>(window);
 7299  				sw->setState(current == "true");
 7300  				s->addWithLabel(CloudText::fieldLabel(fieldName), sw);
 7301  				sw->setOnChangedCallback([values, fieldName, sw]
 7302  				{
 7303  					(*values)[fieldName] = sw->getState() ? "true" : "false";
 7304  				});
 7305  				continue;
 7306  			}
 7307  
 7308  			// Only offer a list when it is short enough to scroll on a handheld.
 7309  			// s3's endpoint carries 351 suggestions and its region 150; those are
 7310  			// effectively free-form and stay as text.
 7311  			if (f.choices > 0 && f.choices <= 12 && !values->count("__edit__"))
 7312  			{
 7313  				std::string choicesArgs = "choices " + cloudShellQuote(backend.name)
 7314  					+ " " + cloudShellQuote(fieldName);
 7315  				if (!backend.subprovider.empty())
 7316  					choicesArgs += " " + cloudShellQuote(backend.subprovider);
 7317  
 7318  				auto list = std::make_shared<OptionListComponent<std::string>>(
 7319  					window, CloudText::fieldLabel(fieldName), false);
 7320  				bool any = false;
 7321  				for (auto& line : cloudRemoteLines(choicesArgs))
 7322  				{
 7323  					auto cols = Utils::String::splitAny(line, "\t", false);
 7324  					if (cols.empty() || cols[0].empty())
 7325  						continue;
 7326  					std::string value = cols[0];
 7327  					std::string label = cloudTsvField(cols, 1).empty() ? value : cols[1];
 7328  					list->add(label, value, current == value);
 7329  					any = true;
 7330  				}
 7331  				if (any)
 7332  				{
 7333  					// Seed the map with whatever the list starts on, and keep
 7334  					// it current as the choice changes.
 7335  					//
 7336  					// NOT addSaveFunc: those run from GuiSettings::save(),
 7337  					// which happens when the page closes. The CONNECT row builds
 7338  					// its command while the page is still open, so a value
 7339  					// left to save() never reaches it -- the chosen vendor was
 7340  					// dropped, silently and with a success dialog, because
 7341  					// WebDAV tolerates an empty one. On Sharepoint or
 7342  					// Nextcloud that is the wrong dialect, not a no-op.
 7343  					// getSelected() asserts that exactly one entry is
 7344  					// selected and abort()s otherwise, and nothing is
 7345  					// selected when the option has no default and so no
 7346  					// entry matched. getSelectedIndex() returns -1 instead.
 7347  					// Leaving it unset is right: we should not invent a
 7348  					// vendor the player did not choose.
 7349  					if (list->getSelectedIndex() >= 0)
 7350  						(*values)[fieldName] = list->getSelected();
 7351  					list->setSelectedChangedCallback(
 7352  						[values, fieldName](const std::string& picked)
 7353  						{
 7354  							(*values)[fieldName] = picked;
 7355  						});
 7356  					s->addWithLabel(CloudText::fieldLabel(fieldName), list);
 7357  					continue;
 7358  				}
 7359  			}
 7360  
 7361  			s->addInputTextRow(CloudText::fieldLabel(fieldName), current, mask,
 7362  				cloudRemoteEditor(values, fieldName),
 7363  				[values, fieldName](const std::string& newVal)
 7364  				{
 7365  					(*values)[fieldName] = newVal;
 7366  				});
 7367  		}
 7368  	}

 7370  	s->addGroup(_("FINISH"));
 7371  	s->addEntry(values->count("__edit__") ? _("SAVE CONNECTION") : _("CONNECT"), true, [window, backend, values, s]
 7372  	{
 7373  		std::string name = Utils::String::trim((*values)["__name__"]);
 7374  		if (name.empty())
 7375  		{
 7376  			window->pushGui(new GuiMsgBox(window,
 7377  				_("GIVE THE CONNECTION A NAME FIRST."), _("OK"), nullptr));
 7378  			return;
 7379  		}
 7380  
 7381  		std::string cmd = std::string(values->count("__edit__") ? "update " : "create ") + cloudShellQuote(name) + " "
 7382  			+ cloudShellQuote(backend.name);
 7383  		// The sub-provider is a real config value, not just a filter.
 7384  		if (!backend.subprovider.empty())
 7385  			cmd += " " + cloudShellQuote("provider=" + backend.subprovider);
 7386  		for (auto& kv : *values)
 7387  		{
 7388  			if (Utils::String::startsWith(kv.first, "__") || Utils::String::trim(kv.second).empty())
 7389  				continue;
 7390  			cmd += " " + cloudShellQuote(kv.first + "=" + kv.second);
 7391  		}
 7392  
 7393  		window->pushGui(new GuiMsgBox(window,
 7394  			values->count("__edit__")
 7395  				? _("UPDATE THIS CONNECTION AND KEEP YOUR SELECTED FOLDERS?\n\nYOUR CURRENT SETTINGS ARE KEPT UNTIL THE NEW ONES WORK.")
 7396  				: _("CONNECT TO THIS PROVIDER NOW?\n\nTHE CONNECTION IS ONLY SAVED IF IT ANSWERS."),
 7397  			values->count("__edit__") ? _("UPDATE CONNECTION") : _("CONNECT"), [window, cmd, s]
 7398  			{
 7399  				// cloud_remote verifies private staged settings before publication;
 7400  				// failure keeps the existing connection. Its two subprocesses
 7401  				// are each bounded to 40 seconds, below this outer deadline. Behind GuiLoading, because that round trip
 7402  				// ran in this callback on the interface thread (fork #103);
 7403  				// the outer box is for an rclone that outlives its own flags.
 7404  				window->pushGui(new GuiLoading<std::string>(window, _("CONNECTING..."),
 7405  					[cmd](IGuiLoadingHandler*)
 7406  					{
 7407  						std::string out = Utils::Platform::GetShOutput(
 7408  							"timeout 100 /usr/bin/cloud_remote " + cmd + " 2>&1");
 7409  						// Masked: the command carries the form's pass=, key=*** and
 7410  						// token=*** values, and a script that echoes its arguments
 7411  						// in a usage or an error would put them in es_log.txt
 7412  						// (#308 8-es claude F-ES-21; fork #177's rule, every
 7413  						// logged command line).
 7414  						LOG(LogInfo) << "cloud_remote create: " << Utils::String::maskSecrets(out);
 7415  						return out;
 7416  					},
 7417  					[window, s](std::string out)
 7418  					{
 7419  						if (out.find("OK=") != std::string::npos)
 7420  						{
 7421  							const size_t start = out.find("OK=") + 3;
 7422  							const std::string remote = Utils::String::trim(out.substr(start, out.find_first_of("\r\n", start) - start));
 7423  							cloudSetupShowDoneStep(window, remote, s);
 7424  							return;
 7425  						}

 7427  						// typo in a key far more easily than they can act on

 7431  								cloudConnectionError(out),

 7433  					}));
 7434  			},

 7438  	// prev->close(), never delete: the window owns it and is mid-input on it.

 7442  // Several backends ask which vendor you are using before their other
 7443  // options mean anything: koofr carries three separate options all named
 7444  // `password` (Koofr, Digi Storage, other) and s3 has 51 vendors with
 7445  // options like ibm_api_key that are noise unless you picked IBMCOS.
 7446  // Asking first keeps the form to the questions that apply.
 7447  static void cloudRemoteChooseBackend(Window* window, const CloudBackend& backend)
 7448  {
 7449  	std::vector<std::pair<std::string, std::string>> subs;
 7450  	for (auto& line : cloudRemoteLines("subproviders " + cloudShellQuote(backend.name)))
 7451  	{
 7452  		auto cols = Utils::String::splitAny(line, "\t", false);
 7453  		if (cols.size() < 1 || cols[0].empty())
 7454  			continue;
 7455  		subs.push_back({ cols[0], cloudTsvField(cols, 1).empty() ? cols[0] : cols[1] });
 7456  	}
 7457  
 7458  	if (subs.empty())
 7459  	{
 7460  		auto values = std::make_shared<std::map<std::string, std::string>>();
 7461  		cloudRemoteShowForm(window, backend, values, nullptr);
 7462  		return;
 7463  	}
 7464  
 7465  	auto s = new GuiSettings(window, _("CONNECT CLOUD STORAGE"));
 7466  	s->setSubTitle(_("WHICH SERVICE?"));
 7467  	for (auto& sub : subs)
 7468  	{
 7469  		CloudBackend chosen = backend;
 7470  		chosen.subprovider = sub.first;
 7471  		s->addEntry(Utils::String::toUpper(sub.second), true, [window, chosen]
 7472  		{
 7473  			auto values = std::make_shared<std::map<std::string, std::string>>();
 7474  			cloudRemoteShowForm(window, chosen, values, nullptr);
 7475  		});
 7476  	}
 7477  	cloudSetupPresent(window, s, nullptr);
 7478  }
 7479  
 7480  // Sign in to a provider using a phone, with nothing else involved.
 7481  //
 7482  // cloud_oauth starts rclone's own authorize flow, reads the provider's real
 7483  // sign-in URL out of it, and serves that on the LAN behind a PIN. All this
 7484  // page does is start it, show where to go, and ask rclone afterwards whether
 7485  // it worked -- the same shape as the SSH wizard's CONTINUE gate, which exists
 7486  // because a page that assumes success is how a failed setup gets reported as
```


## es-app/src/guis/GuiMenu.cpp @ 16dbac5211: lines 7499-8250 (excerpt of 11747)

```text
 7499  static void cloudOAuthPresentChoice(Window* window, const CloudBackend& backend,
 7500  	const std::string& remoteName, GuiSettings* prev,
 7501  	const CloudOAuthReady& ready);
 7502  static void cloudOAuthShowConnected(Window* window, const CloudBackend& backend,
 7503  	const std::string& remoteName, GuiSettings* prev);
 7504  static void cloudOAuthChooseInput(Window* window, const CloudBackend& backend,
 7505  	const std::string& remoteName, GuiSettings* prev);
 7506  
 7507  static void cloudOAuthStart(Window* window, const CloudBackend& backend,
 7508  	const std::string& remoteName, GuiSettings* prev)
 7509  {
 7510  	// Clear the previous attempt's state first, and wait for it: a finished
 7511  	// session leaves its address and PIN behind, and the poll below would
 7512  	// read those and put a dead code on the screen. Doing it here rather
 7513  	// than inside the detached launch means it has already happened before
 7514  	// the first poll, so there is no window where the old one is readable.
 7515  	Utils::Platform::runSystemCommand("/usr/bin/cloud_oauth cancel", "", nullptr);
 7516  
 7517  	// Detached, because it stays up until someone signs in or it times out.
 7518  	// Its own output carries the URL and PIN, which `cloud_oauth info` reads
 7519  	// back rather than this parsing them out of a launch.
 7520  	// --label carries the name we already show in our own menu, so the page
 7521  	// on the phone says "Connect Dropbox" rather than "Connect dropbox".
 7522  	// rclone's backend names are lowercase identifiers, and a page headed
 7523  	// with one reads like a typo on the screen where somebody is deciding
 7524  	// whether to trust us with their storage.
 7525  	std::string cmd = "setsid sh -c " + cloudShellQuote(
 7526  		"/usr/bin/cloud_oauth serve " + cloudShellQuote(backend.name)
 7527  		+ " --name " + cloudShellQuote(remoteName)
 7528  		+ " --label " + cloudShellQuote(backend.label)
 7529  		+ (backend.reauthorize ? " --replace" : "")
 7530  		+ " --timeout 900 >/dev/null 2>&1") + " </dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1 &";
 7531  	Utils::Platform::runSystemCommand(cmd, "", nullptr);
 7532  
 7533  	cloudOAuthChooseInput(window, backend, remoteName, prev);
 7534  }
 7535  
 7536  // Wait for the listener to say it is *waiting*, not merely for an address to
 7537  // appear: a URL on its own proves nothing about whose session it belongs to,
 7538  // which is how a previous attempt's PIN once reached the screen.
 7539  static bool cloudOAuthAwaitSession(std::string& url, bool& onDevice,
 7540  	std::string& exitHint)
 7541  {
 7542  	url.clear();
 7543  	onDevice = false;
 7544  	// A sane default for an image whose cloud_oauth predates EXIT_HINT.
 7545  	// Select exists on every pad we ship for; Mode does not.
 7546  	exitHint = "SELECT + START";
 7547  	bool understood = true;
 7548  
 7549  	// Sixty rather than twenty-four: this runs on a worker thread now, so
 7550  	// patience costs a spinner rather than a frozen screen, and a second
 7551  	// attempt after a cancel was timing out at the old limit.
 7552  	for (int attempt = 0; attempt < 60 && understood; attempt++)
 7553  	{
 7554  		std::string status;
 7555  		url.clear();
 7556  
 7557  		auto reported = Utils::Platform::GetShOutputLines("/usr/bin/cloud_oauth info");
 7558  		// An older script answers `info` with its usage on stderr and nothing
 7559  		// on stdout. Fall through to `url` at once rather than spending the
 7560  		// whole poll waiting for a command that will never answer.
 7561  		understood = !reported.empty();
 7562  
 7563  		for (auto& line : reported)
 7564  		{
 7565  			const std::string trimmed = Utils::String::trim(line);
 7566  			if (Utils::String::startsWith(trimmed, "URL="))
 7567  				url = trimmed.substr(4);
 7568  			else if (Utils::String::startsWith(trimmed, "STATUS="))
 7569  				status = trimmed.substr(7);
 7570  			else if (Utils::String::startsWith(trimmed, "ON_DEVICE="))
 7571  				onDevice = trimmed.substr(10) == "yes";
 7572  			// Which two buttons leave differs by handheld, so the device is
 7573  			// asked rather than the string being written here.
 7574  			else if (Utils::String::startsWith(trimmed, "EXIT_HINT="))
 7575  			{
 7576  				const std::string reported = Utils::String::trim(trimmed.substr(10));
 7577  				if (!reported.empty())
 7578  					exitHint = reported;
 7579  			}
 7580  		}
 7581  
 7582  		if (status == "waiting" && !url.empty())
 7583  			return true;
 7584  
 7585  		url.clear();
 7586  		// A sleep, not a shell running sleep (#308 8-es claude F-ES-16): a
 7587  		// fork and exec every poll for nothing, and runSystemCommand is not
 7588  		// a promise to wait for its child.
 7589  		std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::milliseconds(500));
 7590  	}
 7591  
 7592  	// A script that answered `info` and never said waiting has had its say:
 7593  	// failed, or not yet, for the whole poll. The address `url` still holds
 7594  	// then is not this session's (#308 8a gpt F-ES-18) -- the case the wait
 7595  	// above exists to rule out. Fail closed.
 7596  	if (understood)
 7597  		return false;
 7598  
 7599  	// An image whose cloud_oauth predates `info` still answers `url`.
 7600  	auto lines = Utils::Platform::GetShOutputLines("/usr/bin/cloud_oauth url");
 7601  	if (!lines.empty())
 7602  		url = Utils::String::trim(lines.front());
 7603  	return !url.empty();
 7604  }
 7605  
 7606  // How do you want to type?
 7607  //
 7608  // The sign-in page used to open onto the QR screen whichever way you meant to
 7609  // work, so somebody signing in on the handheld met a page about their phone
 7610  // and stopped there. The phone was never the place the sign-in happens -- it
 7611  // is one of two keyboards -- and a screen that leads with a code to scan says
 7612  // the opposite.
 7613  //
 7614  // Asked once, up front, because it is the only thing that differs between the
 7615  // two routes and everything after it follows.
 7616  static void cloudOAuthChooseInput(Window* window, const CloudBackend& backend,
 7617  	const std::string& remoteName, GuiSettings* prev)
 7618  {
 7619  	// Off the UI thread. This waits on a provider round trip and used to run
 7620  	// inline, spawning a subprocess per poll, so ES painted nothing for the
 7621  	// fifteen-odd seconds it took -- indistinguishable from a freeze, and
 7622  	// reported as one. It is also why the wait could not afford to be
 7623  	// patient; now it can.
 7624  	window->pushGui(new GuiLoading<CloudOAuthReady>(window, _("STARTING SIGN-IN"),
 7625  		[](IGuiLoadingHandler*)
 7626  		{
 7627  			CloudOAuthReady ready;
 7628  			ready.started = cloudOAuthAwaitSession(ready.url, ready.onDevice,
 7629  			                                       ready.exitHint);
 7630  			return ready;
 7631  		},
 7632  		[window, backend, remoteName, prev](CloudOAuthReady ready)
 7633  		{
 7634  			cloudOAuthPresentChoice(window, backend, remoteName, prev, ready);
 7635  		}));
 7636  }
 7637  
 7638  static void cloudOAuthPresentChoice(Window* window, const CloudBackend& backend,
 7639  	const std::string& remoteName, GuiSettings* prev,
 7640  	const CloudOAuthReady& ready)
 7641  {
 7642  	// An image that cannot host the page has only one route; asking would be
 7643  	// a question with a single answer.
 7644  	if (!ready.started || !ready.onDevice)
 7645  	{
 7646  		cloudOAuthShowSignIn(window, backend, remoteName, prev, true, ready);
 7647  		return;
 7648  	}
 7649  
 7650  	auto s = new GuiSettings(window, _("CONNECT CLOUD STORAGE"));
 7651  	s->setSubTitle(CloudText::providerSubtitle(backend.name, backend.label));
 7652  
 7653  	// Showing this choice closes prev. Each action must replace this page,
 7654  	// not the provider page that has already been deleted.
 7655  	s->addGroup(_("HOW DO YOU WANT TO TYPE?"));
 7656  	s->addWithDescription(_("WITH THE ON-SCREEN KEYBOARD"),
 7657  		_("WORKED WITH THE D-PAD, ON THIS SCREEN."),
 7658  		nullptr, [window, backend, remoteName, s, ready]
 7659  		{
 7660  			cloudOAuthShowSignIn(window, backend, remoteName, s, false, ready);
 7661  		}, "", false, true);
 7662  
 7663  	s->addWithDescription(_("WITH MY PHONE"),
 7664  		// One line (D-UI-023): the sentence this replaced measured 929 of
 7665  		// 780 px at 1280x800 and 701 of 620 at 640x480 (#308 F-ES-14).
 7666  		_("YOUR PHONE BECOMES THE KEYBOARD. SCAN THE CODE, THEN CONTINUE."),
 7667  		nullptr, [window, backend, remoteName, s, ready]
 7668  		{
 7669  			cloudOAuthShowSignIn(window, backend, remoteName, s, true, ready);
 7670  		}, "", false, true);
 7671  
 7672  	cloudSetupSetButtons(s, nullptr);
 7673  	cloudOAuthOwnSession(s);
 7674  	cloudSetupPresent(window, s, prev);
 7675  }
 7676  
 7677  // Both keyboard routes use the same window and can fail before it opens
 7678  // (fork #570). The page beneath this dialog still owns the authorization.
 7679  // Copy onReturn: checking completion can replace and delete that page.
 7680  static void cloudOAuthOpenSignIn(Window* window, CloudBackend backend,
 7681  	std::string remoteName, GuiSettings* page, bool usePhone,
 7682  	std::function<void()> onReturn)
 7683  {
 7684  	if (ApiSystem::getInstance()->launchCloudSignIn(window, usePhone))
 7685  	{
 7686  		if (onReturn)
 7687  			onReturn();
 7688  		return;
 7689  	}
 7690  
 7691  	// A completed authorization wins even if the window failed to return.
 7692  	const auto lines = Utils::Platform::GetShOutputLines("/usr/bin/cloud_oauth status");
 7693  	const std::string status = lines.empty() ? "" : Utils::String::trim(lines.front());
 7694  	if (status == "signed-in")
 7695  	{
 7696  		cloudOAuthShowConnected(window, backend, remoteName, page);
 7697  		return;
 7698  	}
 7699  
 7700  	// The bool also covers a failed wait, so do not claim every failure was
 7701  	// at startup. No OK action returns to a page still claiming to open.
 7702  	window->pushGui(new GuiMsgBox(window,
 7703  		_("THE SIGN-IN WINDOW DID NOT OPEN OR STOPPED WORKING.\n\nTRY AGAIN, OR CANCEL SIGN-IN."),
 7704  		_("TRY AGAIN"), [window, backend, remoteName, page, usePhone, onReturn]
 7705  		{
 7706  			// Recheck on the press: authorization can finish or expire while
 7707  			// the question is open. A waiting attempt keeps its phone pairing.
 7708  			const auto lines = Utils::Platform::GetShOutputLines("/usr/bin/cloud_oauth status");
 7709  			const std::string status = lines.empty() ? "" : Utils::String::trim(lines.front());
 7710  			if (status == "signed-in")
 7711  				cloudOAuthShowConnected(window, backend, remoteName, page);
 7712  			else if (status == "waiting")
 7713  				cloudOAuthOpenSignIn(window, backend, remoteName, page, usePhone, onReturn);
 7714  			else
 7715  				cloudOAuthStart(window, backend, remoteName, page);
 7716  		},
 7717  		_("CANCEL SIGN-IN"), [page] { page->close(); }, GuiMsgBoxIcon::ICON_ERROR));
 7718  }
 7719  
 7720  static void cloudOAuthShowSignIn(Window* window, CloudBackend backend,
 7721  	std::string remoteName, GuiSettings* prev, bool usePhone,
 7722  	const CloudOAuthReady& ready)
 7723  {
 7724  	auto s = new GuiSettings(window, _("CONNECT CLOUD STORAGE"));
 7725  	s->setSubTitle(CloudText::providerSubtitle(backend.name, backend.label));
 7726  
 7727  	// The address carries its own PIN in its path. ADDRESS and PIN are still
 7728  	// reported by `info` for anything driving this from a shell, but the page
 7729  	// has nothing to do with them separately -- two numbers on one screen is
 7730  	// what made "code" ambiguous the first time.
 7731  	const std::string url = ready.url;
 7732  	const bool onDevice = ready.onDevice;
 7733  	const std::string exitHint = ready.exitHint;
 7734  
 7735  	if (!ready.started)
 7736  	{
 7737  		cloudSetupAddProse(s, window, _("SIGN-IN DID NOT START"),
 7738  			_("THE DEVICE COULD NOT REACH THE PROVIDER. CHECK THE NETWORK AND TRY AGAIN."));
 7739  		// One button, and it goes back. This page used to carry CONTINUE as
 7740  		// well, which offered to continue with a sign-in that had not begun.
 7741  		s->getMenu().clearButtons();
 7742  		s->getMenu().addButton(_("GO BACK"), _("go back"), [s] { s->close(); });
 7743  		cloudOAuthOwnSession(s);
 7744  		cloudSetupPresent(window, s, prev);
 7745  		return;
 7746  	}
 7747  
 7748  	// Typing on the handheld: nothing to read off the screen, nothing to
 7749  	// scan. Open the provider's page and get out of the way. The keyboard
 7750  	// raises itself on the first field, so there is no instruction to give
 7751  	// that the page does not give better.
 7752  	const bool openImmediately = onDevice && !usePhone;
 7753  	if (openImmediately)
 7754  	{
 7755  		cloudSetupAddProse(s, window, _("SIGNING IN"),
 7756  			_("THE PROVIDER'S PAGE IS OPENING. A KEYBOARD APPEARS WHEN YOU PICK A BOX; MOVE WITH THE D-PAD AND CHOOSE WITH A."));
 7757  		cloudSetupAddSpacer(s, window);
 7758  		cloudSetupAddInfoRow(s, window,
 7759  			Utils::String::format(_("TO COME BACK HERE, PRESS %s").c_str(), exitHint.c_str()));
 7760  		cloudSetupAddInfoRow(s, window, _("THEN CHOOSE 'CONTINUE' TO FINISH"));
 7761  	}
 7762  	else
 7763  	{
 7764  		// The phone is a keyboard, not the place the sign-in happens. Saying
 7765  		// "sign in on your phone" sent people looking for a provider page
 7766  		// there; the page is on the handheld either way.
 7767  		const std::string qrPath = "/tmp/cloud-oauth-qr.png";
 7768  		Utils::Platform::runSystemCommand(
 7769  			"rm -f " + qrPath + "; /usr/bin/qrencode -o " + qrPath
 7770  			+ " -s 8 -m 4 --background=FFFFFF --foreground=000000 -l M "
 7771  			+ cloudShellQuote(url), "", nullptr);
 7772  
 7773  		std::vector<std::pair<std::string, bool>> lines;
 7774  		if (onDevice)
 7775  		{
 7776  			s->addGroup(_("USE YOUR PHONE AS A KEYBOARD"));
 7777  			cloudSetupAddSpacer(s, window);
 7778  			lines.push_back(std::make_pair(_("SCAN THIS, OR OPEN"), false));
 7779  			lines.push_back(std::make_pair(url, true));
 7780  			lines.push_back(std::make_pair(_("WHAT YOU TYPE THERE APPEARS HERE"), false));
 7781  		}
 7782  		else
 7783  		{
 7784  			// The older route, for an image that cannot host the page: the
 7785  			// player signs in on their phone and brings an address back.
 7786  			lines.push_back(std::make_pair(_("OPEN THIS IN A BROWSER"), false));
 7787  			lines.push_back(std::make_pair(url, true));
 7788  			lines.push_back(std::make_pair(_("THE LAST 4 DIGITS ARE A PIN"), false));
 7789  			lines.push_back(std::make_pair(_("IT KEEPS OTHERS OFF THIS PAGE"), false));
 7790  		}
 7791  
 7792  		if (Utils::FileSystem::exists(qrPath))
 7793  			cloudSetupAddQrRow(s, window, qrPath, lines);
 7794  		else
 7795  			for (auto& line : lines)
 7796  				cloudSetupAddInfoRow(s, window, line.first, line.second);
 7797  
 7798  		if (onDevice)
 7799  			cloudSetupAddProse(s, window, _("THEN"),
 7800  				_("CONNECT YOUR PHONE FIRST, THEN CHOOSE 'CONTINUE', AND THE PROVIDER'S PAGE OPENS ON THIS SCREEN."));
 7801  		else
 7802  			cloudSetupAddProse(s, window, _("THEN"),
 7803  				_("THAT PAGE WALKS YOU THROUGH SIGNING IN. COME BACK HERE AND SELECT 'CONTINUE' WHEN IT SAYS YOU ARE CONNECTED."));
 7804  	}
 7805  
 7806  	// Ask cloud_oauth, which asks rclone. Nothing here decides on its own
 7807  	// that a sign-in worked.
 7808  	//
 7809  	// Keep this callback separate so a successful phone-window retry runs
 7810  	// the same completion check as its original CONTINUE press.
 7811  	auto finishSignIn = [window, backend, remoteName, s]
 7812  	{
 7813  		auto lines = Utils::Platform::GetShOutputLines("/usr/bin/cloud_oauth status");
 7814  		std::string status = lines.empty() ? "" : Utils::String::trim(lines.front());
 7815  		LOG(LogInfo) << "cloud_oauth status=" << status;
 7816  
 7817  		if (status == "signed-in")
 7818  		{
 7819  			cloudOAuthShowConnected(window, backend, remoteName, s);
 7820  			return;
 7821  		}
 7822  		if (status == "failed")
 7823  		{
 7824  			window->pushGui(new GuiMsgBox(window,
 7825  				_("THE SIGN-IN DID NOT COMPLETE. TRY AGAIN TO START A NEW SIGN-IN."),
 7826  				_("TRY AGAIN"), [window, backend, remoteName, s] { cloudOAuthStart(window, backend, remoteName, s); },
 7827  				_("CANCEL SIGN-IN"), [s] { s->close(); }));
 7828  			return;
 7829  		}
 7830  		window->pushGui(new GuiMsgBox(window,
 7831  			_("STILL WAITING FOR THE SIGN-IN.\n\nFINISH IT, THEN CHOOSE 'CONTINUE' AGAIN."),
 7832  			_("OK"), nullptr,
 7833  			_("CANCEL SIGN-IN"), [window, s]
 7834  			{
 7835  				Utils::Platform::runSystemCommand("/usr/bin/cloud_oauth cancel", "", nullptr);
 7836  				s->close();
 7837  			}));
 7838  	};
 7839  
 7840  	// Native CONTINUE checks completion; phone CONTINUE first opens the
 7841  	// window while the session is waiting. A failed launch owns its dialog
 7842  	// and must not fall through to the generic still-waiting message.
 7843  	const bool openOnContinue = onDevice && usePhone;
 7844  	cloudSetupSetButtons(s, [window, backend, remoteName, s, openOnContinue, finishSignIn]
 7845  	{
 7846  		if (openOnContinue)
 7847  		{
 7848  			auto info = Utils::Platform::GetShOutputLines("/usr/bin/cloud_oauth status");
 7849  			std::string now = info.empty() ? "" : Utils::String::trim(info.front());
 7850  			if (now == "waiting")
 7851  			{
 7852  				cloudOAuthOpenSignIn(window, backend, remoteName, s, true, finishSignIn);
 7853  				return;
 7854  			}
 7855  		}
 7856  		// Completion can delete s and its buttons; own the invocation first.
 7857  		auto finish = finishSignIn;
 7858  		finish();
 7859  	});
 7860  
 7861  	cloudOAuthOwnSession(s);
 7862  	cloudSetupPresent(window, s, prev);
 7863  
 7864  	// After the page is on the stack, never while it is being built: this
 7865  	// blocks for as long as somebody is signing in, and ES is suspended for
 7866  	// the duration. The page has to already be there for ES to come back to.
 7867  	// backend and remoteName are owned values: presenting s deleted prev,
 7868  	// which may have held the callback that supplied them.
 7869  	if (openImmediately)
 7870  		cloudOAuthOpenSignIn(window, backend, remoteName, s, false, nullptr);
 7871  }
 7872  
 7873  // What it means to have connected something, said once, at the only moment
 7874  // anybody is going to read it.
 7875  //
 7876  // The old ending was a one-line dialog -- "CONNECTED. CLOUD TOOLS ARE NOW
 7877  // AVAILABLE" -- which named neither the provider just connected nor anything
 7878  // the player could go and do. Somebody who has just finished a sign-in on a
 7879  // handheld deserves to be told what they now have.
 7880  static void cloudOAuthShowConnected(Window* window, const CloudBackend& backend,
 7881  	const std::string& remoteName, GuiSettings* prev)
 7882  {
 7883  	cloudSetupShowDoneStep(window, remoteName, prev);
 7884  }
 7885  
 7886  // Adding and renewing are distinct before a sign-in begins. A new name is
 7887  // editable and never overwrites an existing connection at the backend.
 7888  static void cloudOAuthAdd(Window* window, const CloudBackend& backend, GuiSettings* prev)
 7889  {
 7890  	auto s = new GuiSettings(window, _("ADD CONNECTION"));
 7891  	auto name = std::make_shared<std::string>(backend.name);
 7892  	std::set<std::string> existing;
 7893  	for (const auto& line : cloudRemoteLines("list"))
 7894  	{
 7895  		auto cols = Utils::String::splitAny(line, "\t", false);
 7896  		if (cols.size() >= 2) existing.insert(cols[0]);
 7897  	}
 7898  	for (int suffix = 2; existing.count(*name); ++suffix)
 7899  		*name = backend.name + std::to_string(suffix);
 7900  	s->addInputTextRow(_("CONNECTION NAME"), *name, false,
 7901  		[name](Window* w, std::string title, std::string /*initial*/,
 7902  			const std::function<void(std::string)>& save)
 7903  		{
 7904  			if (Settings::getInstance()->getBool("UseOSK"))
 7905  				w->pushGui(new GuiTextEditPopupKeyboard(w, title, *name, save, false));
 7906  			else
 7907  				w->pushGui(new GuiTextEditPopup(w, title, *name, save, false));
 7908  		},
 7909  		[name](const std::string& value) { *name = Utils::String::trim(value); });
 7910  	cloudSetupAddProse(s, window, _("ADD A SEPARATE CONNECTION"),
 7911  		_("THIS KEEPS YOUR EXISTING CONNECTIONS. TO RENEW A SIGN-IN, CHOOSE THAT CONNECTION UNDER CLOUD CONNECTIONS."));
 7912  	s->addEntry(_("SIGN IN"), true, [window, backend, name, existing, s]
 7913  	{
 7914  		if (name->empty() || existing.count(*name))
 7915  		{
 7916  			window->pushGui(new GuiMsgBox(window, _("CHOOSE A DIFFERENT CONNECTION NAME."), _("OK")));
 7917  			return;
 7918  		}
 7919  		cloudOAuthStart(window, backend, *name, s);
 7920  	});
 7921  	cloudSetupPresent(window, s, prev);
 7922  }
 7923  
 7924  // A filtered list of backends. `tier` empty means every tier.
 7925  static void cloudRemoteShowList(Window* window, const std::string& title,
 7926  	const std::string& tier, const std::string& textFilter)
 7927  {
 7928  	auto s = new GuiSettings(window, title);
 7929  
 7930  	auto backends = cloudRemoteBackends();
 7931  	int shown = 0;
 7932  	for (auto& b : backends)
 7933  	{
 7934  		if (b.tier == "plumbing")
 7935  			continue;
 7936  		// "needs-browser" spans both tiers that cannot be finished on the
 7937  		// handheld today.
 7938  		if (tier == "needs-browser")
 7939  		{
 7940  			if (b.tier != "oauth")
 7941  				continue;
 7942  		}
 7943  		else if (!tier.empty() && b.tier != tier)
 7944  			continue;
 7945  		if (!textFilter.empty()
 7946  			&& !Utils::String::containsIgnoreCase(b.label, textFilter)
 7947  			&& !Utils::String::containsIgnoreCase(b.name, textFilter))
 7948  			continue;
 7949  
 7950  		CloudBackend backend = b;
 7951  		shown++;
 7952  		// "oauth" means a backend we could drive natively once the
 7953  		// device-flow work lands (#51 P2). Until then it needs a browser
 7954  		// exactly like "fallback" does, and the form would ask a player for
 7955  		// a client_id and a token they have no way to produce. Group it with
 7956  		// what is honest today rather than with what is planned.
 7957  		if (b.tier == "oauth")
 7958  		{
 7959  			// Not "sign in with your phone": the provider's page opens on
 7960  			// this screen, and the phone is only one of the two keyboards
 7961  			// you can type on. Which one is asked after this, so the row
 7962  			// says what kind of sign-in it is and nothing about where.
 7963  			s->addWithDescription(Utils::String::toUpper(b.label),
 7964  				_("OPENS THE PROVIDER'S SIGN-IN PAGE."), nullptr,
 7965  				[window, backend, s] { cloudOAuthAdd(window, backend, s); },
 7966  				"", false, true);
 7967  		}
 7968  		else
 7969  		{
 7970  			s->addEntry(Utils::String::toUpper(b.label), true, [window, backend]
 7971  			{
 7972  				cloudRemoteChooseBackend(window, backend);
 7973  			});
 7974  		}
 7975  	}
 7976  
 7977  	if (shown == 0)
 7978  		s->addEntry(_("NOTHING MATCHED THAT SEARCH"), false, [] {});
 7979  
 7980  	cloudSetupPresent(window, s, nullptr);
 7981  }
 7982  
 7983  static bool cloudConnectionManual(Window* window)
 7984  {
 7985  	if (SystemConf::getInstance()->get("cloudsaves.startup") != "1" &&
 7986  		SystemConf::getInstance()->get("cloudsaves.gameexit") != "1") return true;
 7987  	window->pushGui(new GuiMsgBox(window,
 7988  		_("TURN OFF BOTH AUTOMATIC SAVE SYNC OPTIONS UNDER MANAGE CLOUD STORAGE BEFORE CHANGING THIS CONNECTION."), _("OK")));
 7989  	return false;
 7990  }
 7991  
 7992  static void cloudConnectionChange(Window* window, const std::string& command, GuiSettings* page)
 7993  {
 7994  	window->pushGui(new GuiLoading<std::string>(window, _("SAVING CONNECTION"),
 7995  		[command](IGuiLoadingHandler*) { return Utils::Platform::GetShOutput("timeout 60 /usr/bin/cloud_remote " + command + " 2>&1"); },
 7996  		[window, page](const std::string& result)
 7997  		{
 7998  			if (!Utils::String::startsWith(result, "OK="))
 7999  			{
 8000  				window->pushGui(new GuiMsgBox(window,
 8001  					_("THE CONNECTION COULDN'T BE CHANGED.") + std::string("\n\n") + cloudConnectionError(result), _("OK")));
 8002  				return;
 8003  			}
 8004  			page->close();
 8005  		}));
 8006  }
 8007  
 8008  static void cloudConnectionDetails(Window* window, const std::string& name, const std::string& type, const std::string& subprovider)
 8009  {
 8010  	auto s = new GuiSettings(window, _("CLOUD CONNECTION"));
 8011  	cloudSetupAddFact(s, window, _("CONNECTION NAME"), name);
 8012  	cloudSetupAddFact(s, window, _("PROVIDER"), CloudText::providerLabel(type));
 8013  	s->addEntry(_("CHECK CONNECTION"), true, [window, name]
 8014  	{
 8015  		cloudSetupRunCheck(window, name + ":", [window](int rc, const std::string&)
 8016  		{
 8017  			window->pushGui(new GuiMsgBox(window, rc == 0 ? _("THE CONNECTION ANSWERED.") : _("COULDN'T REACH THIS CONNECTION. CHECK WI-FI OR SIGN IN AGAIN."), _("OK")));
 8018  		});
 8019  	});
 8020  	s->addEntry(_("USE THIS CONNECTION"), true, [window, name, s]
 8021  	{
 8022  		if (!cloudConnectionManual(window)) return;
 8023  		window->pushGui(new GuiMsgBox(window,
 8024  			_("USE THIS CONNECTION FOR CLOUD TOOLS? YOUR SELECTED FOLDER PATHS WILL STAY THE SAME. CHECK THEM BEFORE YOUR NEXT BACKUP OR RESTORE."),
 8025  			_("USE CONNECTION"), [window, name, s] { cloudConnectionChange(window, "select " + cloudShellQuote(name), s); }, _("CANCEL"), nullptr));
 8026  	});
 8027  	for (auto backend : cloudRemoteBackends())
 8028  	{
 8029  		if (backend.name != type) continue;
 8030  		backend.subprovider = subprovider;
 8031  		if (backend.tier == "oauth")
 8032  		{
 8033  			backend.reauthorize = true;
 8034  			s->addEntry(_("SIGN IN AGAIN"), true, [window, backend, name, s]
 8035  			{
 8036  				if (!cloudConnectionManual(window)) return;
 8037  				window->pushGui(new GuiMsgBox(window,
 8038  					_("SIGN IN TO THE SAME CLOUD ACCOUNT AGAIN. YOUR CURRENT SIGN-IN IS KEPT UNTIL THE NEW ONE WORKS. YOUR SELECTED FOLDERS WILL STAY THE SAME."),
 8039  					_("SIGN IN"), [window, backend, name, s] { cloudOAuthStart(window, backend, name, s); }, _("CANCEL"), nullptr));
 8040  			});
 8041  		}
 8042  		else
 8043  			s->addEntry(_("EDIT CONNECTION"), true, [window, backend, name, s]
 8044  			{
 8045  				if (!cloudConnectionManual(window)) return;
 8046  				auto values = std::make_shared<std::map<std::string, std::string>>();
 8047  				(*values)["__name__"] = name;
 8048  				(*values)["__edit__"] = "1";
 8049  				cloudRemoteShowForm(window, backend, values, s);
 8050  			});
 8051  		break;
 8052  	}
 8053  	s->addEntry(_("REMOVE CONNECTION"), true, [window, name, s]
 8054  	{
 8055  		if (!cloudConnectionManual(window)) return;
 8056  		window->pushGui(new GuiMsgBox(window,
 8057  			_("REMOVE THIS CONNECTION FROM THIS DEVICE? YOUR CLOUD FILES STAY WHERE THEY ARE. IF THIS IS YOUR ACTIVE CONNECTION, CHOOSE ANOTHER BEFORE USING CLOUD TOOLS."),
 8058  			_("REMOVE CONNECTION"), [window, name, s] { cloudConnectionChange(window, "remove " + cloudShellQuote(name), s); }, _("CANCEL"), nullptr));
 8059  	});
 8060  	cloudSetupPresent(window, s, nullptr);
 8061  }
 8062  
 8063  void GuiMenu::openCloudConnections(Window* window)
 8064  {
 8065  	auto s = new CloudConnectionsPage(window, _("CLOUD CONNECTIONS"), [window] { GuiMenu::openCloudConnections(window); });
 8066  	const auto rows = cloudRemoteLines("list");
 8067  	const bool readable = !rows.empty() && Utils::String::startsWith(rows.front(), "STATE=");
 8068  	if (!readable)
 8069  		cloudSetupAddProse(s, window, _("CONNECTIONS UNAVAILABLE"), _("YOUR CLOUD CONNECTIONS COULDN'T BE READ. TRY AGAIN."));
 8070  	else if (rows.front() != "STATE=active")
 8071  		cloudSetupAddProse(s, window, _("NO CONNECTION IN USE"), _("CHOOSE A CONNECTION BEFORE USING CLOUD TOOLS."));
 8072  	for (const auto& line : rows)
 8073  	{
 8074  		auto cols = Utils::String::splitAny(line, "\t", false);
 8075  		if (cols.size() < 3) continue;
 8076  		const auto name = cols[0], type = cols[1];
 8077  		const std::string subprovider = cols.size() > 3 ? cols[3] : "";
 8078  		const auto detail = CloudText::providerLabel(type) + (cols[2] == "active" ? " - " + _("IN USE") : "");
 8079  		s->addWithDescription(name, detail, nullptr,
 8080  			[window, name, type, subprovider] { cloudConnectionDetails(window, name, type, subprovider); }, "", false, true);
 8081  	}
 8082  	if (readable) s->addEntry(_("ADD CONNECTION"), true, [window] { GuiMenu::openCloudAddRemote(window); });
 8083  	s->addEntry(_("USE A COMPUTER INSTEAD"), true, [window] { GuiMenu::openCloudSetup(window); });
 8084  	cloudSetupPresent(window, s, nullptr);
 8085  }
 8086  
 8087  void GuiMenu::openCloudAddRemote(Window* window)
 8088  {
 8089  	auto s = new GuiSettings(window, _("CONNECT CLOUD STORAGE"));
 8090  
 8091  	auto backends = cloudRemoteBackends();
 8092  	if (backends.empty())
 8093  	{
 8094  		// cloud_remote asks rclone for this list, so an empty result means
 8095  		// rclone is missing or broken -- not that there are no providers.
 8096  		s->addEntry(_("PROVIDER LIST UNAVAILABLE"), false, [] {});
 8097  		cloudSetupPresent(window, s, nullptr);
 8098  		return;
 8099  	}
 8100  
 8101  	s->addGroup(_("RECOMMENDED"));
 8102  	for (auto& want : CloudText::recommendedProviders())
 8103  	{
 8104  		for (auto& b : backends)
 8105  		{
 8106  			if (b.name != want.first)
 8107  				continue;
 8108  			// Show our short name here; the form still titles itself with
 8109  			// rclone's description, which is useful once you have chosen.
 8110  			// The tier decides what happens next, so the player never has to
 8111  			// know there are two kinds of sign-in before they pick.
 8112  			CloudBackend backend = b;
 8113  			// The list is handed over as the page this replaces, so the flow
 8114  			// closes back to network settings rather than depositing somebody
 8115  			// who has just connected onto the list of things to connect.
 8116  			s->addEntry(_(want.second.c_str()), true, [window, backend, s]
 8117  			{
 8118  				if (backend.tier == "oauth")
 8119  					cloudOAuthAdd(window, backend, s);
 8120  				else
 8121  					cloudRemoteChooseBackend(window, backend);
 8122  			});
 8123  			break;
 8124  		}
 8125  	}
 8126  
 8127  	s->addGroup(_("MORE"));
 8128  	s->addEntry(_("SEARCH PROVIDERS"), true, [window]
 8129  	{
 8130  		auto onSearch = [window](const std::string& term)
 8131  		{
 8132  			if (Utils::String::trim(term).empty())
 8133  				return;
 8134  			cloudRemoteShowList(window, _("SEARCH RESULTS"), "", Utils::String::trim(term));
 8135  		};
 8136  		if (Settings::getInstance()->getBool("UseOSK"))
 8137  			window->pushGui(new GuiTextEditPopupKeyboard(window, _("SEARCH PROVIDERS"), "", onSearch, false));
 8138  		else
 8139  			window->pushGui(new GuiTextEditPopup(window, _("SEARCH PROVIDERS"), "", onSearch, false));
 8140  	});
 8141  
 8142  	// One complete list, not one per tier. Two entries that each showed part
 8143  	// of the catalogue made the recommendations above look like a third
 8144  	// category rather than a shortlist -- and left no single place to answer
 8145  	// "is my provider supported at all?".
 8146  	s->addWithDescription(_("COMPLETE LIST"),
 8147  		_("EVERY PROVIDER RCLONE SUPPORTS."),
 8148  		nullptr, [window] { cloudRemoteShowList(window, _("COMPLETE LIST"), "", ""); },
 8149  		"", false, true);
 8150  
 8151  	// The old SSH wizard, one level down. It is still the only way to reach
 8152  	// rclone's own config for the things a form cannot express -- crypt and
 8153  	// union remotes, or reconnecting one whose sign-in has lapsed -- but it
 8154  	// is no longer a second front door offering the same job as the first.
 8155  	s->addWithDescription(_("USE A COMPUTER INSTEAD"),
 8156  		_("RUN RCLONE'S SETUP OVER SSH. FOR CONNECTIONS THIS PAGE CAN'T MAKE."),
 8157  		nullptr, [window] { GuiMenu::openCloudSetup(window); }, "", false, true);
 8158  
 8159  	cloudSetupPresent(window, s, nullptr);
 8160  }
 8161  
 8162  void GuiMenu::openCloudSetup(Window* window)
 8163  {
 8164  	auto info = cloudSetupInfo();
 8165  	if (info["IP"].empty())
 8166  	{
 8167  		window->pushGui(new GuiMsgBox(window, _("NO NETWORK CONNECTION DETECTED.\n\nCONNECT TO A NETWORK FIRST, THEN TRY AGAIN.")));
 8168  		return;
 8169  	}
 8170  
 8171  	// Remote names from `rclone listremotes` keep their trailing colon.
 8172  	std::vector<std::string> remotes;
 8173  	for (auto& name : Utils::String::split(info["REMOTES"], ' '))
 8174  		if (!Utils::String::trim(name).empty())
 8175  			remotes.push_back(Utils::String::trim(name));
 8176  
 8177  	LOG(LogInfo) << "cloud_setup wizard: entry, remotes=" << remotes.size();
 8178  
 8179  	if (remotes.empty())
 8180  	{
 8181  		cloudSetupShowSshStep(window, CloudSetupMode::FirstRemote, "", "", nullptr);
 8182  		return;
 8183  	}
 8184  
 8185  	auto s = new GuiSettings(window, _("CLOUD STORAGE SETUP"));
 8186  
 8187  	s->addGroup(_("YOUR CONNECTIONS"));
 8188  	for (auto remote : remotes)
 8189  	{
 8190  		s->addEntry(cloudSetupDisplayName(remote) + "  -  " + _("CHECK IT WORKS"), true, [window, remote]
 8191  		{
 8192  			cloudSetupRunCheck(window, remote, [window](int rc, const std::string& name)
 8193  			{
 8194  				if (rc == 0)
 8195  					window->pushGui(new GuiMsgBox(window, _("YOUR CLOUD IS ANSWERING:") + " " + cloudSetupDisplayName(name)));
 8196  				else if (rc == 2)
 8197  					window->pushGui(new GuiMsgBox(window, _("THIS CONNECTION ISN'T ANSWERING:") + " " + cloudSetupDisplayName(name) + "\n\n" + _("USE 'REPAIR A CONNECTION' TO RENEW ITS SIGN-IN.")));
 8198  				else
 8199  					window->pushGui(new GuiMsgBox(window, _("NO CLOUD STORAGE IS SET UP ON THIS DEVICE YET.")));
 8200  			});
 8201  		});
 8202  	}
 8203  
 8204  	s->addGroup(_("WHAT DO YOU WANT TO DO?"));
 8205  	const std::string syncpath = info["SAVES_REMOTE"];
 8206  	cloudSetupAddFact(s, window, _("CLOUD FOLDER"), syncpath, [window, s, syncpath]
 8207  	{
 8208  		cloudOpenFolderSettings(window, [window, s]
 8209  		{
 8210  			GuiMenu::openCloudSetup(window);
 8211  			s->close();
 8212  		});
 8213  	});
 8214  	std::string preexisting = Utils::String::trim(info["REMOTES"]);
 8215  	s->addEntry(_("ADD ANOTHER CONNECTION"), true, [window, s, preexisting]
 8216  	{
 8217  		cloudSetupShowSshStep(window, CloudSetupMode::AddRemote, "", preexisting, s);
 8218  	});
 8219  	s->addEntry(_("REPAIR A CONNECTION"), true, [window, s, remotes]
 8220  	{
 8221  		if (remotes.size() == 1)
 8222  		{
 8223  			cloudSetupShowSshStep(window, CloudSetupMode::RepairRemote, remotes.front(), "", s);
 8224  			return;
 8225  		}
 8226  		auto picker = new GuiSettings(window, _("WHICH CONNECTION?"));
 8227  		for (auto remote : remotes)
 8228  		{
 8229  			picker->addEntry(cloudSetupDisplayName(remote), true, [window, s, picker, remote]
 8230  			{
 8231  				// The chooser (s) is not the page owning this handler,
 8232  				// so closing it here is safe; the picker itself is
 8233  				// replaced by the wizard page.
 8234  				s->close();
 8235  				cloudSetupShowSshStep(window, CloudSetupMode::RepairRemote, remote, "", picker);
 8236  			});
 8237  		}
 8238  		window->pushGui(picker);
 8239  	});
 8240  
 8241  	window->pushGui(s);
 8242  }
 8243  
 8244  // Post-restore credential re-entry. Backups deliberately carry no
 8245  // secrets, so a restored device needs its credentials back; this page
 8246  // shows each one with its verified state and an inline way to fix it.
 8247  //
 8248  // It is a single page rather than a gated wizard on purpose: every item
 8249  // here is independent and optional (a player with no RetroAchievements
 8250  // account should not be walked through a step for it), so there is no
```

```text
 8253  //
 8254  // Wi-Fi comes first because it is the one item other things depend on:
 8255  // the sanitized system.cfg drops `wifi.key`, so a settings restore
 8256  // disconnects Wi-Fi, and the cloud-journey continuation that follows
 8257  // this page needs the network back.
 8258  static void networkApplyWifi(Window* window, const std::string& title,
 8259  	const std::function<bool()>& apply, const std::function<void(bool)>& onDone);
 8260  
 8261  void GuiMenu::openRestoreRelink(Window* window, bool consumeMarker)
 8262  {
 8263  	const std::string restoreMarker = "/storage/.config/.restore-finish-pending";
 8264  
 8265  	auto theme = ThemeData::getMenuTheme();
 8266  	auto s = new GuiSettings(window, _("FINISH RESTORE PROCESS"));
 8267  	s->setSubTitle(_("RE-ENTER THE PASSWORDS BACKUPS DO NOT INCLUDE"));
 8268  
 8269  	// A state row: check-circle when the credential is present, an empty
 8270  	// circle when it is not. Deliberately NOT a warning triangle -- these
 8271  	// rows are a checklist of things a backup cannot carry, so "not set
 8272  	// yet" is the expected state on arrival, and an alert glyph reads as
 8273  	// "something is broken" to someone who has just restored.
 8274  	// Each row carries a description: the label alone does not say why the
 8275  	// item is here or what happens if it is skipped.
 8276  	auto addCredentialRow = [s, window](const std::string& label, const std::string& description,
 8277  	                                    bool ok, const std::function<void()>& action)
 8278  	{
 8279  		s->addWithDescription((ok ? _U("\uF058  ") : _U("\uF10C  ")) + label,
 8280  			description, nullptr, action, "", false, true);
 8281  	};
 8282  
 8283  	auto reopen = [window, consumeMarker]
 8284  	{
 8285  		// Rebuild so every row re-reads live state after an edit.
 8286  		GuiMenu::openRestoreRelink(window, consumeMarker);
 8287  	};
 8288  
 8289  	s->addGroup(_("NETWORK"));
 8290  	const bool online = !ApiSystem::getInstance()->getIpAddress().empty()
 8291  		&& ApiSystem::getInstance()->getIpAddress() != "NOT CONNECTED";
 8292  	addCredentialRow(_("WI-FI PASSWORD"),
 8293  		_("BACKUPS NEVER INCLUDE YOUR WI-FI KEY. RE-ENTER IT TO GET BACK ONLINE."),
 8294  		online, [window, s, reopen]
 8295  	{
 8296  		auto wifi = new GuiSettings(window, _("WI-FI PASSWORD"));
 8297  		wifi->addInputTextConfigRow(_("WI-FI PASSWORD"), "wifi.key", true);
 8298  		// The association behind the spinner every other Wi-Fi apply in this
 8299  		// file uses (networkApplyWifi): it ran from a save function on the
 8300  		// interface thread, and wifictl connect can take the better part of
 8301  		// two minutes -- a frozen screen after a restore, with no word (#308
 8302  		// 8-es claude F-ES-02, 8a gpt F-ES-07). The page comes back when it
 8303  		// ends, and a failure is said over it.
 8304  		// The key row writes SystemConf in memory only, and the page has no
 8305  		// save function to write the file (GuiSettings::save returns early
 8306  		// without one): written here, before the association reads it.
 8307  		wifi->onFinalize([window, s, reopen]
 8308  		{
 8309  			SystemConf::getInstance()->saveSystemConf();
 8310  			s->close();
 8311  			const std::string ssid = SystemConf::getInstance()->get("wifi.ssid");
 8312  			const std::string key = SystemConf::getInstance()->get("wifi.key");
 8313  			const std::string country = SystemConf::getInstance()->get("wifi.country");
 8314  			if (!SystemConf::getInstance()->getBool("wifi.enabled") || ssid.empty() || key.empty())
 8315  			{
 8316  				reopen();
 8317  				return;
 8318  			}
 8319  			LOG(LogInfo) << "restore relink: reconnecting wifi to " << ssid;
 8320  			networkApplyWifi(window, _("CONNECTING TO WI-FI"),
 8321  				[ssid, key, country] { return ApiSystem::getInstance()->enableWifi(ssid, key, country); },
 8322  				[window, reopen, ssid](bool ok)
 8323  				{
 8324  					reopen();
 8325  					// The picker's words for the same failure (GuiWifi::connect):
 8326  					// WI-FI CONFIGURATION ERROR was upstream's, in a register
 8327  					// a player does not speak (D-UI-031; audit of the fixes, E2
 8328  					// claude G-E2-08).
 8329  					if (!ok)
 8330  						window->pushGui(new GuiMsgBox(window,
 8331  							Utils::String::format(_("COULDN'T CONNECT TO %s.").c_str(), ssid.c_str()) + "\n\n" + _("CHECK THE KEY AND TRY AGAIN.")));
 8332  				});
 8333  		});
 8334  		window->pushGui(wifi);
 8335  	});
 8336  
 8337  	// Account rows appear only when the restored configuration actually
 8338  	// references that service - the username survives a backup, the
 8339  	// password does not, so a username is the signal that an account
 8340  	// exists and needs its password back.
 8341  	const std::string raUser = SystemConf::getInstance()->get("global.retroachievements.username");

 8346  		if (!raUser.empty())
 8347  			addCredentialRow(_("RETROACHIEVEMENTS") + " (" + raUser + ")",
 8348  				_("YOUR USERNAME WAS RESTORED; THE PASSWORD WAS NOT."),
 8349  				!SystemConf::getInstance()->get("global.retroachievements.password").empty(),
 8350  				[window, s, reopen]

 8352  				auto ra = new GuiSettings(window, _("RETROACHIEVEMENTS"));
 8353  				ra->addInputTextConfigRow(_("PASSWORD"), "global.retroachievements.password", true);

 8359  				// save function writes no file (GuiSettings::save).
 8360  				ra->onFinalize([s, reopen] { SystemConf::getInstance()->saveSystemConf(); s->close(); reopen(); });

 8362  			});
 8363

 8365  			addCredentialRow(_("SCREENSCRAPER") + " (" + ssUser + ")",
 8366  				_("YOUR USERNAME WAS RESTORED; THE PASSWORD WAS NOT."),

 8374  				ss->addInputTextConfigRow(_("DEVELOPER PASSWORD"), "ScreenScraperDevPass", true, true);
```

## es-app/src/guis/GuiMenu.cpp @ 16dbac5211: lines 9736-10520 (excerpt of 11747)

```text
 9736  // schedule: ping is time-boxed in ApiSystem::ping (five seconds for the
 9737  // whole sequence); the address query has nothing to put a box around, and
 9738  // if it never returns the row stays blank, which is the truth.
 9739  //
 9740  // Everything the workers touch on the page is held weakly. Closing the page
 9741  // destroys its rows and its list, and a result that arrives afterwards
 9742  // finds nothing to write to. The strings are looked up here, on the
 9743  // interface thread, so the workers carry text and nothing else.
 9744  static void networkSettingsFillIn(Window* window, GuiSettings* s,
 9745  	const std::shared_ptr<TextComponent>& ipRow, const std::shared_ptr<std::vector<std::string>>& ipList,
 9746  	const std::shared_ptr<TextComponent>& statusRow)
 9747  {
 9748  	std::weak_ptr<ComponentList> list = s->getMenu().getList();
 9749  	std::weak_ptr<TextComponent> ip = ipRow;
 9750  	std::weak_ptr<std::vector<std::string>> addresses = ipList;
 9751  	std::weak_ptr<TextComponent> status = statusRow;
 9752  	const std::string connected = _("CONNECTED");
 9753  	const std::string notConnected = _("NOT CONNECTED");
 9754  
 9755  	// A value that changes width after its row was laid out has to be laid
 9756  	// out again, or an address arriving into an empty slot runs off the
 9757  	// right edge of the row: the list positions a row's elements once, when
 9758  	// the row is added, and again only when its size changes.
 9759  	auto fill = [list](const std::weak_ptr<TextComponent>& row, const std::string& text)
 9760  	{
 9761  		auto component = row.lock();
 9762  		auto rows = list.lock();
 9763  		if (component == nullptr || rows == nullptr)
 9764  			return;
 9765  		component->setText(text);
 9766  		rows->onSizeChanged();
 9767  	};
 9768  
 9769  	std::thread([window, ip, addresses, notConnected, fill]
 9770  	{
 9771  		// The row's value is the first address on a link of the device's own
 9772  		// (NOT CONNECTED when there is none, as INTERNET STATUS says beside
 9773  		// it); the list behind A carries every address with its interface, a
 9774  		// tunnel's included and named, since a tailnet address is what the
 9775  		// player reaches the device by from elsewhere (fork #279).
 9776  		const std::vector<Utils::Platform::InterfaceAddress> found = Utils::Platform::queryInterfaceAddresses();
 9777  		std::string first;
 9778  		std::vector<std::string> lines;
 9779  		for (const auto& a : found)
 9780  		{
 9781  			lines.push_back(a.address + "  " + a.interface);
 9782  			if (first.empty() && a.physical)
 9783  				first = a.address;
 9784  		}
 9785  		AppWindow::post(window, [ip, addresses, first, lines, notConnected, fill]
 9786  		{
 9787  			if (auto held = addresses.lock())
 9788  				*held = lines;
 9789  
 9790  			if (first.empty())
 9791  				fill(ip, notConnected);
 9792  			else
 9793  				fill(ip, lines.size() == 1 ? first : first + " (+)");
 9794  		});
 9795  	}).detach();
 9796  
 9797  	std::thread([window, status, connected, notConnected, fill]
 9798  	{
 9799  		const bool online = ApiSystem::getInstance()->ping();
 9800  		AppWindow::post(window, [status, online, connected, notConnected, fill]
 9801  		{
 9802  			fill(status, online ? connected : notConnected);
 9803  		});
 9804  	}).detach();
 9805  }
 9806  
 9807  // The WI-FI NETWORK row's value (WI-FI SSID until D-UI-071): the network the device is on now, from
 9808  // NetworkManager, asked off the interface thread as the IP address and the
 9809  // internet status are (fork #191). It used to be wifi.ssid, the network
 9810  // configured last, because that is what the row edited -- and once
 9811  // autoconnect had joined a saved network the two differed, so the RG SP
 9812  // showed a setting as though it were the connection. Maintainer,
 9813  // 2026-09-15: "Why won't the right-hand value always be the network you're
 9814  // connected to? This matches the paradigm on other operating systems,
 9815  // phones, etc., where it shows the currently connected network, and then
 9816  // you can choose to connect to another network." So the value is the
 9817  // connection: the network, NOT CONNECTED when there is none, COULDN'T CHECK
 9818  // when NetworkManager did not answer (a silence is not "not connected"),
 9819  // CHECKING... until the answer is in. A opens the picker (GuiWifi), whose
 9820  // press connects or joins and has this page rebuilt to read the result
 9821  // back. The setting still exists and still follows the player's choice; it
 9822  // is just not what this row shows.
 9823  static void networkSettingsFillInSsid(Window* window, GuiSettings* s, const std::shared_ptr<TextComponent>& valueRow)
 9824  {
 9825  	std::weak_ptr<ComponentList> list = s->getMenu().getList();
 9826  	std::weak_ptr<TextComponent> value = valueRow;
 9827  	const std::string notConnected = _("NOT CONNECTED");
 9828  	const std::string couldNotCheck = _("COULDN'T CHECK");
 9829  
 9830  	std::thread([window, list, value, notConnected, couldNotCheck]
 9831  	{
 9832  		std::string joined;
 9833  		const bool answered = ApiSystem::getInstance()->getCurrentWifiSsid(joined);
 9834  		const std::string text = !answered ? couldNotCheck : (joined.empty() ? notConnected : joined);
 9835  		AppWindow::post(window, [list, value, text]
 9836  		{
 9837  			// A value that changes width after its row was laid out has to
 9838  			// be laid out again (networkSettingsFillIn). Both are held
 9839  			// weakly: the page may be gone before the answer is.
 9840  			auto component = value.lock();
 9841  			auto rows = list.lock();
 9842  			if (component == nullptr || rows == nullptr)
 9843  				return;
 9844  			component->setText(text);
 9845  			rows->onSizeChanged();
 9846  		});
 9847  	}).detach();
 9848  }
 9849  
 9850  // Connect or disconnect Wi-Fi off the interface thread. wifictl connect can
 9851  // take the better part of two minutes on a healthy stack (ApiSystem::
 9852  // enableWifi says why), and every caller of this is a menu callback: the
 9853  // screen froze for the whole association, which on a handheld reads as a
 9854  // crash. GuiLoading keeps a spinner drawing and hands the result back on
 9855  // the interface thread; nothing reaches the page beneath it meanwhile, so a
 9856  // caller that captured the page still has it when onDone runs.
 9857  static void networkApplyWifi(Window* window, const std::string& title,
 9858  	const std::function<bool()>& apply, const std::function<void(bool)>& onDone)
 9859  {
 9860  	window->pushGui(new GuiLoading<bool>(window, title,
 9861  		[apply](IGuiLoadingHandler*) { return apply(); },
 9862  		[onDone](bool ok)
 9863  		{
 9864  			if (onDone != nullptr)
 9865  				onDone(ok);
 9866  		}));
 9867  }
 9868

 9870  {

 9902  	});

 9937  #ifdef RK3399

10364  // MANAGE SAVED NETWORKS (fork #191): the networks NetworkManager saved --
10365  // one profile per network the device has joined, autoconnected to whichever
10366  // is in range -- with the one in use marked, and A to forget one. Without
10367  // this page a network joined once was joined again on every boot it was in
10368  // range for, with nothing on the device to say so or to stop it. "Saved" is
10369  // the maintainer's word for them (2026-09-15) and wifictl's; the page, its
10370  // group and its dialogs use no other.
10371  
10372  // The help bar says what A does here: FORGET, and only while there is a
10373  // network to forget -- a prompt for a key that does nothing is worse than
10374  // none (es-code-traps).
10375  class GuiManageNetworks : public GuiSettings
10376  {
10377  public:
10378  	GuiManageNetworks(Window* window, bool hasNetworks)
10379  		: GuiSettings(window, _("MANAGE SAVED NETWORKS")), mHasNetworks(hasNetworks) { }
10380  
10381  	std::vector<HelpPrompt> getHelpPrompts() override
10382  	{
10383  		std::vector<HelpPrompt> prompts;
10384  		for (const auto& prompt : GuiSettings::getHelpPrompts())
10385  			if (prompt.first != BUTTON_OK)
10386  				prompts.push_back(prompt);
10387  		if (mHasNetworks)
10388  			prompts.push_back(HelpPrompt(BUTTON_OK, _("FORGET")));
10389  		return prompts;
10390  	}
10391  
10392  private:
10393  	bool mHasNetworks;
10394  };
10395  
10396  // A press on a network: the question names it and says what changes -- the
10397  // device stops joining it on its own, and, for the one in use, drops it now.
10398  // YES first, NO last so B answers NO (es-ui-style-guide). The delete is one
10399  // nmcli call, bounded in ApiSystem, behind the spinner; onForgotten then
10400  // rebuilds the caller's page from NetworkManager so every row reads the
10401  // list as it is now (the manage page reopens itself, the Wi-Fi picker
10402  // refreshes, #318), and the toast over it says what happened, disconnection
10403  // included.
10404  void GuiMenu::forgetWifiNetworkWithConfirmation(Window* window, const std::string& name, bool inUse, const std::function<void()>& onForgotten)
10405  {
10406  	std::string text = Utils::String::format(_("FORGET %s?").c_str(), name.c_str()) + "\n\n";
10407  	if (inUse)
10408  		text += _("YOU'RE CONNECTED TO IT NOW, SO YOU'LL BE DISCONNECTED.") + " ";
10409  	text += _("THIS DEVICE WON'T JOIN IT AGAIN ON ITS OWN.");
10410  
10411  	window->pushGui(new GuiMsgBox(window, text, _("YES"), [window, name, onForgotten]
10412  	{
10413  		LOG(LogInfo) << "forget network: forgetting " << name;
10414  		window->pushGui(new GuiLoading<std::pair<bool, bool>>(window, _("PLEASE WAIT"),
10415  			[name](IGuiLoadingHandler*)
10416  			{
10417  				bool disconnected = false;
10418  				const bool forgotten = ApiSystem::getInstance()->forgetWifiNetwork(name, disconnected);
10419  				return std::make_pair(forgotten, disconnected);
10420  			},
10421  			[window, name, onForgotten](std::pair<bool, bool> result)
10422  			{
10423  				if (!result.first)
10424  				{
10425  					LOG(LogWarning) << "forget network: could not forget " << name;
10426  					window->pushGui(new GuiMsgBox(window, _("COULDN'T FORGET") + " " + name + ". " + _("TRY AGAIN."), _("OK")));
10427  					return;
10428  				}
10429  				LOG(LogInfo) << "forget network: forgot " << name << (result.second ? ", disconnected" : "");
10430  				if (onForgotten)
10431  					onForgotten();
10432  				window->displayNotificationMessage(_U("\uF058  ") + name + " : "
10433  					+ (result.second ? _("FORGOTTEN, AND YOU'RE DISCONNECTED") : _("FORGOTTEN")));
10434  			}));
10435  	}, _("NO"), nullptr));
10436  }
10437  
10438  static void manageNetworksAddRow(Window* window, GuiSettings* page, const WifiText::SavedNetwork& network)
10439  {
10440  	auto theme = ThemeData::getMenuTheme();
10441  	ComponentListRow row;
10442  
10443  	// The name as NetworkManager has it. A network's name is case-sensitive
10444  	// and is what the player recognises it by, so the row is built by hand:
10445  	// addWithLabel and addWithDescription upper-case their label.
10446  	auto name = std::make_shared<TextComponent>(window, network.name, theme->Text.font, theme->Text.color);
10447  	if (EsLocale::isRTL())
10448  		name->setHorizontalAlignment(Alignment::ALIGN_RIGHT);
10449  	row.addElement(name, true);
10450  
10451  	// The mark, on the right, at the row's own weight: a fact beside an
10452  	// action rather than a line under it, so every row stays one line high.
10453  	// CONNECTED, the word the Wi-Fi picker beside this page and its toast
10454  	// use for the same fact; it read IN USE (#308 2-wifi claude F-WF-11,
10455  	// least-surprise.md: same thing, same words).
10456  	if (network.inUse)
10457  	{
10458  		const std::string connected = _("CONNECTED");
10459  		auto mark = std::make_shared<TextComponent>(window, connected, theme->Text.font, theme->Text.color, Alignment::ALIGN_RIGHT);
10460  		mark->setSize(theme->Text.font->sizeText(connected + "  ").x(), 0);
10461  		row.addElement(mark, false);
10462  	}
10463  
10464  	const std::string networkName = network.name;
10465  	const bool inUse = network.inUse;
10466  	row.makeAcceptInputHandler([window, page, networkName, inUse]
10467  	{
10468  		GuiMenu::forgetWifiNetworkWithConfirmation(window, networkName, inUse, [window, page]
10469  		{
10470  			delete page;
10471  			GuiMenu::openManageNetworks(window);
10472  		});
10473  	});
10474  	page->addRow(row);
10475  }
10476  
10477  static void manageNetworksShow(Window* window, const std::vector<WifiText::SavedNetwork>& networks)
10478  {
10479  	auto page = new GuiManageNetworks(window, !networks.empty());
10480  	page->addGroup(_("SAVED NETWORKS"));
10481  	if (networks.empty())
10482  		page->addEntry(_("NO SAVED NETWORKS"), false);
10483  	for (const auto& network : networks)
10484  		manageNetworksAddRow(window, page, network);
10485  	window->pushGui(page);
10486  }
10487  
10488  void GuiMenu::openManageNetworks(Window* window)
10489  {
10490  	// The list is one nmcli call, but over D-Bus to a NetworkManager that
10491  	// can stop answering (fork #102), so it is fetched behind the spinner
10492  	// with ApiSystem's bound rather than in a page's constructor. A list
10493  	// that could not be read is a dialog, not an empty page: NO SAVED
10494  	// NETWORKS is a fact about the device, and that would be a guess.
10495  	window->pushGui(new GuiLoading<std::pair<bool, std::vector<WifiText::SavedNetwork>>>(window, _("PLEASE WAIT"),
10496  		[](IGuiLoadingHandler*)
10497  		{
10498  			std::vector<WifiText::SavedNetwork> networks;
10499  			const bool readable = ApiSystem::getInstance()->getSavedWifiNetworks(networks);
10500  			return std::make_pair(readable, networks);
10501  		},
10502  		[window](std::pair<bool, std::vector<WifiText::SavedNetwork>> result)
10503  		{
10504  			if (!result.first)
10505  			{
10506  				LOG(LogWarning) << "manage networks: the saved networks could not be read";
10507  				window->pushGui(new GuiMsgBox(window, _("COULDN'T READ THE SAVED NETWORKS. TRY AGAIN."), _("OK")));
10508  				return;
10509  			}
10510  			manageNetworksShow(window, result.second);
10511  		}));
10512  }
```

```text
  391  std::shared_ptr<SwitchComponent> GuiSettings::addSwitch(const std::string& title, const std::string& description, const std::string& settingsID, bool storeInSettings, const std::function<void()>& onChanged)
  392  {

  394

  412  			onChanged();
  413  	});
  414  
  415  	return comp;
```

```text
  168  			static void add(const std::string& key, bool exists, bool dir, bool symlink = false)
  169  			{
  170  				if (!Settings::UseFileCache())
  171  					return;
  172  
  173  				std::unique_lock<std::shared_mutex> guard(mFileCacheMutex);
  174  				auto [it, inserted] = mFileCache.try_emplace(hashPath(key), exists, dir, symlink);
  175  				if (!inserted)
  176  				{
  177  					it->second._exists = exists;
  178  					it->second._directory = dir;
  179  					it->second._symlink = symlink;
  180  				}
  181  			}
  182  
  183  			static void remove(const std::string& key)
  184  			{
  185  				if (!Settings::UseFileCache())
  186  					return;
  187  
  188  				std::unique_lock<std::shared_mutex> guard(mFileCacheMutex);
  189  
  190  				mFileCache.erase(hashPath(key));
  191  				
  192  				auto parent = Utils::FileSystem::getParent(key);
  193  				while (!parent.empty())
  194  				{
  195  					mFileCache.erase(hashPath(parent + "/*"));
  196  					parent = Utils::FileSystem::getParent(parent);
  197  				}
  198  			}

  200  			template<typename Func>
  201  			static std::optional<bool> queryCacheEntry(const std::string& key, Func func)
  202  			{
  203  				if (!Settings::UseFileCache())
  204  					return std::nullopt;
  205  
  206  				auto hash = hashPath(key);
  207  				{
  208  					std::shared_lock<std::shared_mutex> lock(mFileCacheMutex);
  209  					if (auto* e = getCacheEntry(key, hash))
  210  						return func(e);
  211  				}
  212  
  213  				auto fetched = statKey(key);
  214  				std::unique_lock<std::shared_mutex> lock(mFileCacheMutex);
  215  				return func(&insertEntry(hash, key, std::move(fetched)));
  216  			}

  218  			static std::optional<bool> exists(const std::string& key)

  223  			// Non-blocking probe: returns true if the key's existence is already
  224  			// recorded in the cache (either exists or known-not-to-exist).
  225  			// Never calls stat64 — safe to call from the render thread.
  226  			static bool isCached(const std::string& key)
  227  			{
  228  				if (!Settings::UseFileCache())
```

```text
  853  		int runSystemCommand(const std::string& cmd_utf8, const std::string& name, Window* window)
  854  		{
  855  			LOG(LogInfo) << "runSystemCommand: " + Utils::String::maskSecrets(cmd_utf8);
  856  
  857  			// Parent fork
  858  			pid_t ret = fork();
  859  			if (ret == 0)
  860  			{
  861  				ret = fork();
  862  				if (ret == 0)
  863  				{
  864  					execl("/usr/bin/sh", "sh", "-c", (cmd_utf8).c_str(), (char *) NULL);
  865  					_exit(1); // execl failed
  866  				}
  867  				int status;
  868  				waitpid(ret, &status, 0); // Wait for fork exit..
  869  				_exit(0);
  870  			}
  871  			else
  872  			{
  873  				if (ret > 0)
  874  				{
  875  					int status;
  876  					waitpid(ret, &status, 0); // keep calm and kill zombies
  877  				}
  878  			}
  879  			return 0;
  880  		}

  891  		std::string GetShOutput(const std::string& mStr)
  892  		{
  893  			std::string result, file;
  894  			FILE* pipe{popen(mStr.c_str(), "r")};
  895  			char buffer[256];
  896  
  897  			while(fgets(buffer, sizeof(buffer), pipe) != NULL)
  898  			{
  899  				file = buffer;
  900  				result += file.substr(0, file.size() - 1);
  901  			}
  902  
  903  			pclose(pipe);
  904  			return result;
  905  		}
  906  
  907  		std::vector<std::string> GetShOutputLines(const std::string& mStr)
  908  		{
  909  			std::vector<std::string> lines;
  910  
  911  			FILE* pipe = popen(mStr.c_str(), "r");
  912  			if (pipe == nullptr)
  913  				return lines;
  914  
  915  			// Accumulate rather than treating each fgets() as a line: a line
  916  			// longer than the buffer arrives in several reads, and only the
  917  			// last of them carries the newline.
  918  			std::string pending;
  919  			char buffer[512];
  920  			while (fgets(buffer, sizeof(buffer), pipe) != NULL)
  921  			{
  922  				pending += buffer;
  923  				size_t nl;
  924  				while ((nl = pending.find('\n')) != std::string::npos)
  925  				{
  926  					lines.push_back(pending.substr(0, nl));
  927  					pending.erase(0, nl + 1);
  928  				}
  929  			}
  930  			if (!pending.empty())
  931  				lines.push_back(pending);
  932  
  933  			pclose(pipe);
  934  			return lines;
  935  		}
  936  
  937  		unsigned long long getTotalSystemMemory() 
  938  		{

  941  			status.dwLength = sizeof(status);
  942  			if (GlobalMemoryStatusEx(&status))
  943  				return status.ullTotalPhys;
```

```text
  425  
  426  		std::string shellQuote(const std::string& _string)
  427  		{
  428  			// Wrap the value in single quotes so it reaches the command as one
  429  			// literal argument. Inside single quotes the shell expands nothing,
  430  			// so $, `, \, " and whitespace survive untouched; a single quote
  431  			// itself has to leave the quoted run, be escaped, and re-enter it.
  432  			std::string quoted = "'";
  433  
  434  			for (auto character : _string)
  435  			{
  436  				if (character == '\'')
  437  					quoted += "'\\''";
  438  				else
  439  					quoted += character;
  440  			}
  441  
  442  			return quoted + "'";
  443  
  444  		} // shellQuote
  445  
  446  		// ------------------------------------------------------------ maskSecrets
  447  		//
  448  		// A credential's value never reaches a log (D-INFRA-011): every command
  449  		// line, URL or query EmulationStation logs goes through here first, at
  450  		// every level (fork #177). The key or flag stays and the value reads
  451  		// <redacted>, so the line still says what ran; an empty value stays
  452  		// empty, since whether a credential is set may be shown and only what it
  453  		// is may not. A value already written as a placeholder in angle brackets
```
