```text
  244  def cmd_list():
  245      _, config = connections.read_config()
  246      try:
  247          selected = connections.active(config)
  248      except connections.Refused:
  249          selected = ""
  250      print("STATE=" + ("active" if selected else "unselected"))
  251      for name in sorted(config.sections()):
  252          print("%s\t%s\t%s\t%s" % (name, config.get(name, "type", fallback=""),
  253                                      "active" if name == selected else "saved", config.get(name, "provider", fallback="")))
  254      return 0
  255  
  256  
  257  def cmd_create(name, backend_type, settings, replace=False):
  258      providers = load_providers()
  259      if backend_type not in {p.get("Name") for p in providers}:
  260          raise connections.Refused("Unknown provider.")
  261      if not replace and backend_type in ("s3", "gcs", "azureblob") and not any(k == "directory_markers" for k, _ in settings):
  262          settings.append(("directory_markers", "true"))
  263      log("updating remote " + name if replace else "creating remote " + name)
  264      connections.save(name, backend_type, settings, replace=replace)
  265      print("OK=%s" % name)
  266      return 0
  267  
  268  
  269  def usage():
  270      sys.stderr.write(
  271          "usage: cloud_remote providers\n"
  272          "       cloud_remote subproviders <type>\n"
  273          "       cloud_remote fields <type> [subprovider]\n"
  274          "       cloud_remote choices <type> <field> [subprovider]\n"
  275          "       cloud_remote create <name> <type> [key=*** ...]\n"
  276          "       cloud_remote update <name> <type> [key=*** ...]\n"
  277          "       cloud_remote active\n"
  278          "       cloud_remote select|remove <name>\n"
  279          "       cloud_remote list\n")
  280      return 1
  281  
  282  
  283  def main(argv):
  284      if len(argv) < 2:
  285          return usage()
  286      command = argv[1]
  287  
  288      if command == "providers":
  289          return cmd_providers()
  290  
  291      if command == "subproviders":
  292          if len(argv) != 3:
  293              return usage()
  294          return cmd_subproviders(argv[2])
  295  
  296      if command == "choices":
  297          if len(argv) not in (4, 5):
  298              return usage()
  299          return cmd_choices(argv[2], argv[3], argv[4] if len(argv) == 5 else "")
  300  
  301      if command == "fields":
  302          if len(argv) not in (3, 4):
  303              return usage()
  304          return cmd_fields(argv[2], argv[3] if len(argv) == 4 else "")
  305  
  306      if command == "list":
  307          return cmd_list()
  308  
  309      if command == "selection-reason" and len(argv) == 2:
  310          print(connections.selection_reason())
  311          return 0
  312  
  313      if command == "active":
  314          name = connections.active()
  315          if name:
  316              print(name + ":")
  317              return 0
  318          return 1
  319  
  320      if command in ("select", "remove") and len(argv) == 3:
  321          (connections.select if command == "select" else connections.remove)(argv[2])
  322          print("OK=" + argv[2])
  323          return 0
  324  
  325      if command in ("create", "update"):
  326          if len(argv) < 4:
  327              return usage()
  328          settings = []
  329          for pair in argv[4:]:
  330              if "=" not in pair:
  331                  sys.stderr.write("expected key=*** got: %s\n" % pair)
  332                  return 1
  333              key, value = pair.split("=", 1)
  334              settings.append((key, value))
  335          return cmd_create(argv[2], argv[3], settings, replace=(command == "update"))
  336  
  337      return usage()
  338  
  339  
  340  if __name__ == "__main__":
  341      try:
  342          sys.exit(main(sys.argv))
  343      except (connections.Refused, OSError) as exc:
  344          sys.stderr.write(str(exc) + "\n" if isinstance(exc, connections.Refused) else "Cloud settings could not be read or saved.\n")
  345          sys.exit(2)
```

## setrootpass @ 8ebaf8daad: lines 1-65 (excerpt of 65)

```text
    1  #!/bin/bash
    2  # SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0
    3  # Copyright (C) 2023 JELOS (https://github.com/JustEnoughLinuxOS)
    4  
    5  . /etc/profile
    6  
    7  ROOTPASS="$1"
    8  
    9  # An absent setting means "not provided" -- never "the password is the empty
   10  # string". backuptool strips credentials from the archived system.cfg on
   11  # purpose, so on the first boot after a restore `get_setting root.password`
   12  # is empty and 007-rootpw called this with "". Applying that set an empty
   13  # password across /storage/.cache/shadow, Samba and Syncthing, and wrote
   14  # root.password=*** back to system.cfg -- on a device that rejoins wifi
   15  # moments later, with SSH, Samba, Syncthing and simple-http-server (which
   16  # serves all of /storage) all authenticating against it.
   17  #
   18  # The shadow file lives on /storage and is never captured by a backup, so
   19  # refusing here leaves the password the device already had: a restore becomes
   20  # invisible with respect to the device password, which is the outcome we
   21  # want. The post-restore page still offers to set one.
   22  if [ -z "${ROOTPASS}" ]
   23  then
   24    logger -t setrootpass "refused an empty password; leaving the existing one in place"
   25    echo "setrootpass: refusing to set an empty password; the existing one is unchanged" >&2
   26    exit 1
   27  fi
   28  
   29  LPC=$(awk -F: '/^root/{print $3}' /storage/.cache/shadow)
   30  CURRENT=$(awk -F: '/^root/{print $2}' /storage/.cache/shadow)
   31  
   32  # Turn it into a usable hash
   33  NEW=$(cryptpw -m sha512 "${ROOTPASS}" 2>/dev/null)
   34  
   35  # Set the root password.
   36  sed -i "s#root:${CURRENT}:${LPC}::::::#root:${NEW}:${LPC}::::::#g" /storage/.cache/shadow 2>/dev/null
   37  
   38  # Create a root user and set the password.
   39  # printf, not echo -ne: a backslash in the password must reach smbpasswd as typed (fork #198).
   40  printf '%s\n%s\n' "${ROOTPASS}" "${ROOTPASS}" | smbpasswd -sa root 2>/dev/null
   41  
   42  # Set the root user and password for SyncThing
   43  syncthing generate --gui-user "root" --gui-password "${ROOTPASS}" >/dev/null 2>&1
   44  
   45  # Save the password so we can display it in ES
   46  set_setting root.password "${ROOTPASS}"
   47  
   48  # Restart syncthing if it was enabled.
   49  SYNCSTATUS=$(get_setting syncthing.enabled)
   50  SYNCACTIVE=$(systemctl status syncthing | awk '/active/ {print $2}')
   51  if [ "${SYNCSTATUS}" = "1" ] &&
   52     [ "${SYNCACTIVE}" = "active" ]
   53  then
   54    systemctl restart syncthing >/dev/null 2>&1
   55  fi
   56  
   57  # Restart simple-http-server if it is enabled
   58  SIMPLESTATUS=$(get_setting simplehttp.enabled)
   59  SIMPLEACTIVE=$(systemctl status simple-http-server | awk '/active/ {print $2}')
   60  if [ "${SIMPLESTATUS}" = "1" ] &&
   61     [ "${SIMPLEACTIVE}" = "active" ]
   62  then
   63    systemctl restart simple-http-server >/dev/null 2>&1
   64  fi
   65  
```

```text
  125  function get_setting() {
  126    if [ -n "${3}" ]
  127    then
  128      ### Test to see if we have a game setting.
  129      VAR="$2\[\"$(echo ${3} | sed -E "s~'~\\\x27~g"';s~[()&]~\\&~g')\"\]\.$1"
  130      OUTPUT=$(awk 'BEGIN {FS="="} /^'"${VAR}"'/ {print $NF}' ${J_CONF})
  131      if [ ! -z "${OUTPUT}" ]
  132      then
  133        echo ${OUTPUT}
  134        return
  135      else
  136        ### If not, check to see if we have a system setting.
  137        LOCAL=$(awk -F: '/^'"${2}.${1}"'=/ { st = index($0,"=");print substr($0,st+1);exit}' ${J_CONF})
  138        if [ ! -z "${LOCAL}" ]
  139        then
  140          echo ${LOCAL}
  141          return
  142        fi
  143      fi
  144    fi
  145  
  146    if [ -z "${3}" ] && [ -n "${2}" ]
  147    then
  148      ### Check to see if we have a global setting.
  149      LOCAL=$(awk -F: '/^'"${2}.${1}"'=/ {  st = index($0,"=");print substr($0,st+1);exit}' ${J_CONF})
  150      if [ ! -z "${LOCAL}" ]
  151      then
  152        echo ${LOCAL}
  153        return
  154      fi
  155    fi
  156  
  157    ### Check to see if we have a "system." global setting.
  158    SYSTEM=$(awk -F: '/^system.'"${1}"='/ { st = index($0,"=");print substr($0,st+1);exit}' ${J_CONF})
  159    if [ -n "${SYSTEM}" ]
  160    then
  161      echo ${SYSTEM}
  162      return
  163    fi
  164  
  165    ### Check to see if we have a "global." global setting."
  166    LOCAL=$(awk -F: '/^'"${1}"='/ { st = index($0,"=");print substr($0,st+1);exit}' ${J_CONF})
  167    if [ -z "${LOCAL}" ]
  168    then
  169      awk -F: '/^global.'"${1}"='/ { st = index($0,"=");print substr($0,st+1);exit}' ${J_CONF}
  170      return
  171    else
  172      echo ${LOCAL}
  173    fi
  174    return
  175  }

  178  # (CONFIG_LOGGER=y); should an image lack it, stderr is the next best reader.
  179  function wait_lock_say() {
  180    if command -v logger >/dev/null 2>&1
  181    then
  182      logger -t wait_lock "${*}"
  183    else
```
